To ensure reproducibility, all training code, evaluation scripts, and model weights have been made publicly available.

</details>


### [38] [The Last Visible Pixel: Probing Fine-Scale Perception in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07861)
*Lujun Li,Lama Sleem,Niccolo Gentile,Yangjie Xu,Yewei Song,Wenbo Wu,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FineSightBench基准，系统评估VLM在4-48px尺度下的细粒度视觉感知与推理能力，发现感知在12px左右饱和，而推理能力即使在较大尺度下仍存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在多模态理解方面表现出色，但其细粒度视觉感知能力尚未被充分探索。类似于“Strawberry中有多少个r”的问题自然延伸为：VLM能可靠感知的最小视觉模式是多大？作者希望系统性地探究这一极限。

Method: 构建FineSightBench基准，将感知任务（像素级识别字母、形状、物体）与推理任务（空间推理、计数、排序）分离，在4-48px的受控尺度下进行系统评估，并结合详细的失败模式分析。

Result: 感知能力在约12px时饱和，而推理能力即使在更大尺度下仍然受限，存在持续的计数和序列错误。展示了感知与推理之间显著的解离现象。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在细尺度视觉推理方面存在根本性缺陷，亟需更严格的评估标准。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) excel at multimodal understanding and reasoning, yet their fine-grained visual perception remains underexplored. A natural extension of ``How many r are there in Strawberry?'' asks: how small a visual pattern can a VLM reliably perceive? As such, we introduce FineSightBench, a new benchmark that systematically probes this limit by separating perception tasks (pixel-level recognition of letters, shapes, objects) from reasoning tasks (spatial reasoning, counting, ordering over small targets) across controlled scales of 4--48px. Through comprehensive experiments and detailed failure mode analysis on state-of-the-art models, we reveal a sharp dissociation: perception saturates around 12px, while reasoning remains limited even at larger scales, with persistent numeracy and sequence errors. These findings expose fundamental deficiencies in VLMs' fine-scale visual reasoning that demand more rigorous evaluation.

</details>


### [39] [VisualFLIP: Do Predictions Depend on Task-Critical Visual Evidence in Multimodal Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07872)
*Didi Zhu,Changrui Chen,Stefanos Zafeiriou,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisualFLIP是一个包含1,374张图片的配对基准测试，通过保持问题不变但最小化改变关键视觉证据使正确答案翻转，来评估多模态大语言模型是否真正基于视觉证据推理。实验发现，模型正确率与证据依赖性是不同维度，许多模型在视觉变化后无法更新答案，出现"坍塌"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型即使回答正确，其预测可能并未基于任务关键的视觉证据，正确率无法反映推理是否真正"接地"于视觉信息。

Method: 构建VisualFLIP基准，包含1,374张图像，按基数、属性、空间和逻辑任务组织为同题扰动配对（相同问题，最小改变关键证据，正确答案确定性地翻转）。使用配对准确率（要求模型正确回答配对两侧）和坍塌率（模型对两侧给出相同非空答案的频率）进行评估。

Result: 配对正确率与证据依赖性虽相关但本质不同；即使能力强的模型也可能在关键视觉变化后仍无法更新答案；部分模型在顺序设置中（已看到前一图像答案后）坍塌问题更严重。

Conclusion: 仅靠准确率不足以评估多模态模型的视觉接地能力，VisualFLIP的配对指标揭示了正确率无法反映的推理缺陷。

Abstract: When a multimodal large language model answers a visual reasoning question correctly, is the prediction actually supported by the task-critical visual evidence? Correct answers can coexist with flawed reasoning, making accuracy alone an incomplete test of grounding. We introduce VisualFLIP, a paired benchmark with 1,374 images arranged as same-question perturbation pairs across cardinality, attribute, spatial, and logic tasks. Each pair keeps the question fixed but minimally changes the evidence so the gold answer deterministically flips. We evaluate 24 MLLMs with pair accuracy, which requires solving both sides of a pair, and Collapse Rate (CR), which measures how often a model that solves at least one side repeats the same non-empty answer for both images. Together, these metrics show that paired correctness and evidence dependence are related but distinct: capable models can still fail to update after task-critical visual changes, and collapse becomes more severe for some models when the edited image follows an earlier answer in a sequential setting. Further details are available on our project page: https://didizhu-judy.github.io/VisualFLIP/

</details>


### [40] [The Cross-Architecture Substrate: A Domain-Transcendent, Calibration-Surviving Geometric Invariant of Modern Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07882)
*Yousef Radwan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 13种现代视觉编码器的前16个主方向收敛到相同的16维几何结构（跨架构基板），该基板跨域迁移、非随机、早期出现，并可用于无标签迁移性过滤、域检测、低样本探测和无需教师的蒸馏辅助，但不跨模态且不预测迁移质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同训练目标（分类、对比、重构、图像-文本匹配）的视觉神经网络内部表征是否真的如预期般不同。

Method: 对13种现代视觉编码器进行PCA、中心核对齐（CKA）和Pang 2026校准分析，识别前16个主方向并研究其跨架构、跨域的一致性。

Result: 跨架构基板存在：跨4域中位Procrustes-CKA 0.679，跨8域0.604；非像素统计（0.263）、非Gabor特征（0.31）、非随机投影（0.041）；训练前10%即出现；四项应用：无标签迁移性过滤（比LogME快3倍，+0.15 Kendall-tau）、四域检测器（99.6%）、16维低样本探测（比768维DINOv2高3.78pp）、无需教师的蒸馏辅助（峰值增益7.56pp）。

Conclusion: 不同视觉编码器存在共享的低维几何结构，该结构可用于多项下游任务，但不跨模态，不预测迁移质量，也不支持跨范式蒸馏。

Abstract: Different vision neural networks -- trained to classify, contrast, reconstruct, or match images to text -- should have correspondingly different internal representations. We report that they do not. After training, the top sixteen principal directions of variation inside thirteen modern vision encoders converge to the same sixteen-dimensional geometric object. We call this the cross-architecture substrate and study it with PCA, centred kernel alignment (CKA), and Pang 2026 calibration. The substrate transports across four visual domains (natural photographs, medical CT, satellite, microscopy) at median Procrustes-CKA 0.679, and across eight domains (adding sketches, depth, thermal infrared, astronomy) at 0.604, every pair >0.40. It survives Pang calibration globally (7.4x disc-vs-MAE separation, n=13,394) and locally (4.82-5.30, p<10^{-44}). It is not pixel statistics (0.263), not Gabor features (0.31), not a random projection (0.041), and emerges in the first 10% of training while accuracy keeps climbing. We deliver four applications: a label-free transferability filter beating LogME (3x faster, +0.15 Kendall-tau); a four-way domain detector (99.6% accuracy); a frozen low-shot probe (16 dims beat 768-dim DINOv2 by 3.78pp at N=50 labels per class); and a teacher-free distillation auxiliary matching trained-teacher KD on 33 pairs (7.56pp peak gain at 10% label fraction). The substrate does not cross modalities, does not help cross-paradigm distillation, and does not predict transfer quality (rho=0.08 against transfer accuracy).

</details>


### [41] [C3VD-DEFCOL: A Deformable Colonoscopy Dataset with Time-Resolved 3D Ground Truth and Realistic Appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07891)
*Ethan Luk,Mayank V. Golhar,Anthony Song,Raúl Iranzo,Víctor M. Batlle,Lalithkumar Seenivasan,José M. M. Montiel,Nicholas J. Durr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C3VD-DEFCOL是一个用于评估可变形结肠镜3D重建的框架与数据集，它结合了真实的几何结构与逼真的体内外观纹理，通过可控变形（包括蠕动波和中心线运动）生成带有配对3D真值的视频数据，并利用LTX-2.3模型实现仿真到真实的图像翻译。该数据集包含110个视频、11种结肠网格几何结构，提供三种蠕动严重程度作为受控评估轴，可用于测试变形3D重建算法中的位姿估计等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏同时具备逼真体内外观和密集时间分辨3D真值的数据集，尤其是在非刚性变形条件下，这限制了结肠镜3D重建算法的开发与评估。

Method: 基于C3VD/C3VDv2结肠网格和相机轨迹，生成可控的表面变形（蠕动波和中心线运动），渲染每帧深度、法线、光流、位姿和3D网格，再利用渲染的几何信息（主要是深度）作为条件，通过LTX-2.3仿真到真实翻译模型生成具有体内黏膜外观的RGB视频。

Result: 生成了包含110个视频、11种结肠网格几何结构的数据集，提供三种蠕动严重程度；通过外观真实性、几何一致性和时间一致性指标评估生成视频，并利用配对真值对变形3D重建中的位姿估计任务进行基准测试，验证了位姿估计误差随变形严重程度增加而增大。

Conclusion: C3VD-DEFCOL作为一个可重复、定量的评估平台，用于测试可变形3D重建算法，旨在缩小合成数据集与体内结肠镜之间的领域差距。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction could improve colonoscopy by estimating mucosal coverage and alerting clinicians to missed regions during screening. However, algorithm development is limited as no current datasets provide both a realistic in vivo appearance and dense, time-resolved 3D ground truth, especially under non-rigid deformation. We present C3VD-DEFCOL, a framework and dataset for evaluating deformable colonoscopy reconstruction with paired geometry and realistic texture. Starting from C3VD/C3VDv2 colon meshes and camera trajectories, we generate controlled deformations of the colon surface, including peristaltic waves and centerline motion, and render per-frame depth, surface normals, optical flow, camera poses, and time-stamped 3D meshes. We then use the rendered geometry, primarily depth, to condition an LTX-2.3-based sim-to-real translation model that produces RGB clips with in vivo-like mucosal color, texture, vasculature, and specular appearance while preserving the underlying 3D scene structure. The resulting dataset contains 110 videos from 11 unique colon mesh geometries, with varying camera trajectories, appearances, and parameterized deformation regimes, including three peristaltic severity levels that serve as controlled evaluation axes. We evaluate the generated videos using appearance realism, geometric consistency, and temporal consistency metrics, and use the paired ground truth to benchmark the downstream task of pose estimation in deformable 3D reconstruction. Our experiments show how pose estimation error increases with increasing deformation severity, providing a controlled stress test that is not possible with existing in vivo datasets. Overall, C3VD-DEFCOL is designed as a reproducible, quantitative evaluation platform for testing deformable 3D reconstruction algorithms, with the goal of reducing the domain gap between synthetic datasets and in vivo colonoscopy.

</details>


### [42] [TBD-VLA: Temporal Block Diffusion Vision Language Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07895)
*Sung-Wook Lee,Xuhui Kang,Yen-Ling Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TBD-VLA提出了一种基于块扩散的离散视觉-语言-动作框架，通过时序块划分和掩码离散扩散实现并行动作解码与时间自回归的统一，在仿真和真实操作任务中显著优于先前VLA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散VLA模型将动作生成视为下一个token预测，存在高推理延迟且忽视动作轨迹中的时序结构；最近并行解码虽提升效率，但缺乏对token依赖关系的显式建模。

Method: 将动作序列划分为时序块，在块内执行掩码离散扩散（并行解码），块间保持自回归生成；额外支持通过时序修补实现动作块异步执行（如实时分块）。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务中显著优于先前VLA方法，兼具强时间连贯性和提升的推理速度。

Conclusion: TBD-VLA为构建快速、具有时间感知能力的离散VLA模型提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Discrete Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically formulate action generation as next-token prediction over discretized action spaces, conditioning each token autoregressively on prior context. While effective, this paradigm incurs high inference latency and largely ignores the temporal structure inherent in action trajectories. Recent efforts introduce parallel decoding to improve efficiency, enabling faster inference, but lack explicit mechanisms for modeling token dependencies. We introduce TBD-VLA, a discrete token-based VLA framework that incorporates block diffusion to enable temporal action generation. We partition action sequences into temporal blocks and perform masked discrete diffusion within each block, while maintaining autoregressive generation across blocks. This design unifies temporal autoregression and parallel action decoding, achieving both strong temporal coherence and improved inference speed. In addition, the explicit temporal modeling enables asynchronous execution of action chunks (e.g., Real-Time Chunking) via temporal in-painting. TBD-VLA significantly outperforms prior VLA approaches in both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, offering a scalable path toward fast, temporally aware, discrete VLA models. Project webpage: https://tbd-vla.github.io/

</details>


### [43] [3D Oral Modelling with Improved Vertex Distribution Using Matching-Based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07907)
*Jihun Cho,Soo-Yeon Jeong,Eun-Jeong Bae,Sun-Young Ihm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对先前3D口内重建模型中预测顶点集中在高密度区域的问题，提出改进的损失函数，引入带过滤的匈牙利匹配和排斥损失，使顶点分布更均匀，尽管准确率从77.49%降至68.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于深度学习的3D口内重建模型预测的顶点集中在真实值的高密度区域，其他区域几乎未被覆盖，导致重建完整性不足。

Method: 在原有MobileNetV2+Multi-head Attention框架基础上，改进损失函数，引入带过滤的匈牙利匹配（Hungarian matching with filtering）和排斥损失（Repulsion Loss），以强制预测顶点在重建模型上更均匀分布。

Result: 模型准确率为68.02%，数值上低于先前模型的77.49%，但顶点聚类问题显著缓解，预测顶点在整个重建表面分布更加均匀。

Conclusion: 通过牺牲一定的数值准确率，改进的损失函数有效解决了顶点分布不均匀问题，提升了3D口内重建的完整性和均匀性。

Abstract: In our previous work, a deep learning-based framework for 3D intraoral reconstruction was proposed. The model directly predicts explicit 3D point cloud coordinates from ten fixed-angle intraoral images, employing MobileNetV2 and Multi-head Attention for multi-view feature fusion, with a combined L1 Loss and Chamfer Distance as the loss function. Although the model achieved an accuracy of 77.49%, predicted vertices tended to concentrate in high-density regions of the ground truth, leaving other regions largely uncovered.
  In this paper, an improved loss function is proposed to address this limitation. Hungarian matching with filtering and Repulsion Loss are introduced to enforce more uniform vertex distribution across the reconstructed model. The proposed model achieves an accuracy of 68.02%, which is numerically lower than the previous model. However, the vertex clustering issue observed in the prior work is substantially alleviated, with predicted vertices distributed more evenly across the entire reconstructed surface.

</details>


### [44] [Decoupling Semantics and Logic: A Training-Free Coarse-to-Fine Pipeline for Video Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07924)
*Jiaxin Dai,Zehang Wei,Jiamin Yan,Xiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的两阶段级联视频RAG流水线，通过解耦语义检索与认知推理，结合高保真语义预取模块和基于LLM的自适应迭代过滤代理，实现了跨语言长视频理解、严格角色遵从和零幻觉时间定位，并在MAGMaR竞赛中取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨语言长视频理解中语义检索与逻辑推理耦合困难、角色一致性难以保持以及时间定位存在幻觉等关键挑战。

Method: 采用无需训练的两阶段级联架构：第一阶段通过高召回语义预取模块，仅使用高保真视觉摘要和全局文本描述进行密集检索，隔离OCR/ASR等噪声模态；第二阶段利用商业LLM驱动的自适应迭代推理过滤代理，重新引入完整多模态上下文进行精细认知重排序，并使用提示塑形机制约束生成器输出严格格式化的JSON响应及精确块级引用。

Result: 在MAGMaR RAG赛道评估中，该方法在信息检索和角色条件生成方面均表现出卓越的精确度。

Conclusion: 解耦语义检索与逻辑推理的级联架构能够有效提升跨语言长视频理解的质量，无需训练即可实现精准的检索与生成，是一种资源高效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents our system description for the 2nd Workshop on Multimodal Augmented Generation via MultimodAl Retrieval (MAGMaR). Addressing the critical challenges of cross-lingual long-video comprehension, strict persona adherence, and zero-hallucination temporal grounding, we propose a fully training-free, two-stage cascaded Video RAG pipeline. Our architecture strategically decouples semantic retrieval from cognitive logical reasoning through a modality-aware division of labor. In the first stage, a high-recall semantic pre-fetching module employs dense retrieval using only high-fidelity visual summaries and global text descriptions, explicitly isolating noisy modalities (e.g., OCR and ASR) to maintain a pristine vector space. In the second stage, an Adaptive, Iterative, and Reasoning-based (A.I.R.) filtering agent, powered by a commercial Large Language Model (LLM), performs fine-grained cognitive reranking. The agent re-incorporates full multimodal contexts to enforce strict logical alignment with user personas, effectively pruning semantically similar but logically irrelevant candidates. Finally, a Prompt Sculpting mechanism constrains the generator to synthesize the distilled subset into strictly formatted JSON responses with exact chunk-level citations. Evaluated on the RAG track, our resource-aware approach shows exceptional precision in both information retrieval and persona-conditioned generation.

</details>


### [45] [LEGS: Laplacian-Enhanced Gaussian Splatting with a Nonlinear Weighted Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07932)
*Yongfei Guo,Qizhou Huo,Xuan Sun,Yuanhao Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LEGS，一种基于拉普拉斯增强的高斯泼溅方法，通过二阶拉普拉斯结构引导和非线性响应-权重映射函数替代一阶梯度引导，在不改变原始3DGS渲染管线的前提下提升结构感知优化，在Tanks&Temples和Mip-NeRF360数据集上PSNR最高提升1.68 dB，并可作为通用损失级扩展应用于FastGS和FasterGS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS的光度损失对平坦区域和结构丰富区域一视同仁，限制了锐利轮廓和精细细节的恢复；已有的EGGS方法虽引入边缘引导加权，但依赖一阶梯度响应和线性加权，结构感知能力仍有限。

Method: 将一阶梯度引导替换为二阶拉普拉斯结构引导，通过非线性响应-权重映射函数将归一化拉普拉斯响应转换为像素级权重，构建非线性的加权损失函数，保持原始3DGS渲染管线不变。

Result: 在完整Tanks&Temples和Mip-NeRF360数据集上，LEGS相比3DGS PSNR最高提升1.68 dB，相比EGGS最高提升0.52 dB；将所提二阶非线性加权策略集成到FastGS和FasterGS中，PSNR最高提升1.69 dB。

Conclusion: LEGS作为通用损失级扩展，能有效提升多种高斯泼溅管线的结构感知能力，在AR/VR、沉浸式可视化和实时3D内容生成中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become an efficient explicit representation for radiance field reconstruction and real-time novel view synthesis. However, its standard photometric loss treats flat and structure-rich regions similarly, which may limit the recovery of sharp contours and fine details. Edge-Guided Gaussian Splatting (EGGS) improves structure awareness through edge-guided weighting, but mainly relies on first-order gradient responses and linear weighting. In this paper, we propose LEGS, a Laplacian-Enhanced Gaussian Splatting method with a nonlinearly weighted loss. LEGS replaces first-order gradient guidance with second-order Laplacian structural guidance and maps the normalized Laplacian response into pixel-wise weights through nonlinear response-to-weight functions. The proposed loss improves structure-aware Gaussian optimization while keeping the original 3DGS rendering pipeline unchanged. Experiments on the full Tanks\&Temples and Mip-NeRF360 datasets show that LEGS improves peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) by up to 1.68 dB over 3DGS and up to 0.52 dB over EGGS. Incorporating the proposed second-order nonlinear weighting strategy into FastGS and FasterGS further improves PSNR by up to 1.69 dB, demonstrating its effectiveness as a general loss-level extension for Gaussian Splatting pipelines with potential applications in AR/VR, immersive visualization, and real-time 3D content generation.

</details>


### [46] [REACT 2026: The Fourth Multiple Appropriate Facial Reaction Generation Challenge: Personalised MAFRG and Appropriate EEG Reaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07935)
*Siyang Song,Micol Spitale,Zijian Wu,Xiangyu Kong,Cheng Luo,Cristina Palmero,German Barquero,Sergio Escalera,Michel Valstar,Mohamed Daoudi,Fabien Ringeval,Andrew Howes,Elisabeth Andre,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REACT 2026挑战赛提出多模态适切面部反应生成任务，新增个性标签和EEG数据，支持离线和在线两种场景下的通用与个性化面部反应生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二元交互中，针对说话者的行为，听者可能产生多种合适的面部反应。现有模型在生成多样化、个性化且同步的面部反应方面仍存在不足，尤其缺乏结合情感、行为和神经生理信号的个性化建模。

Method: 在MARS数据集基础上新增Big-Five个性标签和EEG记录，定义四个子挑战：离线通用MAFRG、离线个性化MAFRG、在线通用MAFRG、在线个性化MAFRG。提供公开基线模型。

Result: 提出了新的多对一个性化面部反应生成设定，融合表达性行为、情感和神经生理信号。基线代码已公开。

Conclusion: REACT 2026通过引入个性与EEG信号，推动了个性化、多模态、适切的面部反应生成研究，为该领域提供了标准化基准和挑战平台。

Abstract: In dyadic interactions, various human facial reactions could be appropriate for responding to each human speaker behaviour. Following the successful organisation of the REACT 2023, 2024 and 2025 challenge series, a body of generative deep learning (DL) models have been developed for the problem of multiple appropriate facial reaction generation (MAFRG). This year, we propose the REACT 2026 challenge encouraging the development and benchmarking of Machine Learning (ML) models that can generate multiple personalised, appropriate, diverse, realistic and synchronised human-style facial reactions expressed by a specific human listener for responding to each given speaker behaviour. As a key of the challenge, we continuously provide challenge participants with MARS dataset introduced by REACT 2025 but additionally provide individual-level Big-Five personality labels and EEG recordings. This introduces a new one-to-many personalised facial reaction generation setting combining human expressive behavioural, affective and neurophysiological signals, which remains largely unexplored in current dyadic interaction modelling. This paper also presents the challenge guidelines and new baselines on the four proposed sub-challenges: Offline generic and personalised MAFRG as well as Online generic and personalised MAFRG, respectively, which are publicly available at https://github.com/reactmultimodalchallenge/baseline_react2026.

</details>


### [47] [DAL-PCQA: Enabling Distortion-Level and Language-Driven Reasoning for Point Cloud Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07938)
*Swarna Chakraborty,Gabriel De Castro Araújo,Syeda Tasmi Faria,Marcelo M. Carvalho,Mylene C. Q. Farias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DAL-PCQA，一个面向点云质量评估的、包含多级失真标注和结构化语言描述的数据集，旨在使PCQA从单一的MOS预测走向可解释的、基于失真类型的语言驱动评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PCQA方法仅预测单一MOS值，无法揭示感知退化的具体原因；而人类观察者会自然地基于模糊、颜色偏移、点密度变化等特定失真进行推理，现有数据集缺乏这种细粒度的失真感知标注。

Method: 构建DAL-PCQA数据集，在基准点云上增加多级失真严重性标签、离散质量类别以及结构化的自然语言描述；定义包含光度与几何伪影的点云专属失真分类体系；对比零样本与微调多模态模型在生成感知质量描述上的表现。

Result: 失真感知监督显著提升了生成描述与真实标注之间的词汇和语义对齐效果；统计分析揭示了不同失真类型与质量等级下的特征退化模式。

Conclusion: DAL-PCQA通过支持可解释的失真级推理，推动了语言驱动的、可解释的点云质量评估发展。

Abstract: Point Cloud Quality Assessment (PCQA) methods typically predict scalar Mean Opinion Scores (MOS), which quantify overall perceptual degradation but do not reveal its causes. In contrast, human observers naturally reason in terms of specific distortions such as blur, color shifts, point density changes, missing regions, and geometric deformations. To close this gap, we introduce DAL-PCQA, a distortion-aware, language-annotated dataset for PCQA. DAL-PCQA augments benchmark point clouds with multi-level distortion severity labels, discrete quality categories, and structured natural language descriptions aligned with human perception. We define a point-cloud-specific distortion taxonomy that covers both photometric and geometric artifacts. Statistical analysis reveals characteristic degradation patterns across distortion types and quality levels. To assess the utility of these annotations, we compare zero-shot and fine-tuned multimodal models for generating perceptual quality descriptions. Experiments show that distortion-aware supervision substantially improves lexical and semantic alignment with ground-truth descriptions. By enabling interpretable, distortion-level reasoning, DAL-PCQA facilitates language-driven, explainable point cloud quality assessment. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/swarna96/DAL-PCQA.

</details>


### [48] [ChronoPhyBench: Do MLLMs Truly Understand the World or Merely Exploit Language Priors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07962)
*Bin Zhu,Yanhao Jia,Kexin Zhao,Jie Wang,Munan Ning,Hao Li,Yuwei Niu,Tanqing Sun,Huangchong Yan,Mingjun Pan,Xinyi Wu,Qishen Yin,Yunyang Ge,Shuai Zhao,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoPhyBench基准，用于评估多模态大模型是否真正具备跨模态的物理动态推理能力，而非仅依赖语言先验。实验表明，当前开源模型的物理多模态推理能力仍处于初级阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM虽在开放世界推理中表现出色，但存在关键模糊性：模型是真正融合跨模态信息构建物理推理链，还是仅依赖语言先验掩盖单模态依赖、虚高超模态能力？为严格消除语言模态偏置和捷径，亟需建立能够检验真实物理推理能力的基准。

Method: 提出ChronoPhyBench基准，将下一个状态预测与VQA范式统一，基于历史视频上下文和文本描述，强制模型通过单图像选择和更复杂的多帧时序排序来推断后续物理状态。同时构建包含超10,000个长视频和500万token标注的大规模数据集。

Result: 实验评估揭示当前开源模型在物理基础多模态推理上的能力仍处于初级阶段，与以往基准的结论形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 本工作系统性地压力测试多模态模型的推理能力，量化幻觉率，推动物理AI发展，为迈向AGI提供稳健透明的评估框架。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in open-world reasoning and understanding. However, a critical ambiguity persists: it remains unclear whether these models genuinely synthesize cross-modal information to construct physically grounded reasoning chains, or if they merely exploit strong language priors to mask single-modality reliance, thereby hallucinating advanced multimodal capabilities. Motivated by this, and to rigorously mitigate language modality bias and shortcuts, we propose a novel multimodal Chrono}logical Physical Dynamics Reasoning Benchmark ChronoPhyBench, which unifies next state prediction with Visual Question Answering (VQA) paradigms by conditioning on historical video context and textual captions to enforce models to deduce subsequent physical states through both single image selection and the inherently more complex task of multiple frame chronological sorting. Concurrently, we construct a large-scale multimodal reasoning dataset curated using the ChronoPhyBench criteria, comprising over 10,000 long-form videos paired with meticulously annotated captions, totaling 5M tokens. Our experimental evaluations reveal a stark contrast to conclusions drawn by previous benchmarks. The capacity of current open-source models to perform physically grounded multimodal reasoning remains in its infancy. Ultimately, this work seeks to systematically stress-test the reasoning capabilities of multimodal models, quantify hallucination rates, and advance the development of Physical AI, thereby providing the community with a robust and transparent evaluation framework toward Artificial General Intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [49] [DisCo: World Models with Discrete Camera Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07967)
*Hongrui Huang,Junke Wang,Quanhao Li,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DisCo，通过离散动作基元替代连续相机轨迹作为条件，解决可控视频世界模型中动作表示纠缠导致的跟随不可靠问题，并构建DisCoBench基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频世界模型依赖连续相机轨迹作为动作条件，导致不同运动模式下的特征相似度高，动作可控性差，尤其在复杂运动序列中动作跟随不可靠。

Method: 提出DisCo模型，使用一组紧凑的离散动作基元作为生成条件，提升动作表示的可分离性；同时构建DisCoBench基准，涵盖短期、长程和高动态探索场景。

Result: 实验表明DisCo在保持视觉质量的同时，实现了显著更可靠的动作跟随能力。

Conclusion: 动作表示的离散化是提升可控视频世界模型动作跟随可靠性的有效途径，DisCo在多个场景下优于现有方法。

Abstract: Controllable video world models target interactive world exploration, where models must faithfully execute explicit action commands while preserving visual quality and temporal coherence. However, most existing approaches rely on continuous camera trajectories as action conditions, which often lead to unreliable action following, especially under complex motion sequences. In this work, we identify action representation entanglement as a key bottleneck in controllable video generation, and show that continuous camera representations lead to high feature similarity across distinct motion patterns, degrading action controllability. Based on this insight, we propose DisCo, a controllable video world model that conditions generation on a compact set of discrete action primitives to improve action separability. We further introduce DisCoBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating the ability of models in short-term, long-horizon, and highly dynamic exploration scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DisCo achieves significantly more reliable action following while preserving visual quality.

</details>


### [50] [FMRFusion: Frequency-Aware Multi-View Representation Learning for Heterogeneous Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07985)
*Tao Zhoua,Yunlong Liu,Qinghui Chen,Zekai Zhang,Minlong Sun,Changlin Biana,Dagang Li,Wenmin Wang,Jinglin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FMRFusion，一种频率感知的多视图表示学习网络，通过多尺度结构感知模块、双线性频率分解、跨视图互补交互和流匹配技术，实现红外与可见光图像的高质量融合，尤其在夜间场景表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合方法采用单模块堆叠方式提取双模态特征，导致对异构数据特性学习不完整，限制了融合效果和鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 多尺度结构感知模块捕获判别性结构和上下文信息；2) 双线性频率分解机制分离高低频成分，联合建模局部细节与全局表征；3) 跨视图互补交互显式建模反射光与辐射强度响应的互补特性；4) 流匹配渐进式优化融合特征，从粗数据到高质量表示。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，FMRFusion在多种融合任务中取得优越且一致的性能，尤其在夜间场景表现突出。

Conclusion: 提出的频率感知多视图表示学习网络有效解决了异构图像融合中特征学习不完整的问题，为红外与可见光融合提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Infrared and visible image fusion aims to generate a composite image that retains significant target information and preserves detailed textures, integrating two heterogeneous modalities. Previous image fusion methods typically adopt a single-module stacking approach to extract features from the two modalities. However, these approaches may result in incomplete learning of their distinct characteristics, thereby limiting the fusion effectiveness and constrain ing robustness in real-world heterogeneous data scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose FMRFusion, a frequency-aware multi-view representation learning network for Heterogeneous Image Fusion. A Multi-Scale Struc tural Perception Module is introduced to effectively capture discriminative structures, extracting fine-grained local structures and essential contextual information. A bilinear frequency decomposition mechanism is employed to sepa rate features into high-frequency and low-frequency components, enabling joint modeling of local details and global representations across different frequency domains. Moreover, a Cross-View Complementary Interaction is incorpo rated to explicitly model and fuse the complementary characteristics between reflected light information and radiative intensity responses, facilitating effective cross-view interaction. We further improve the Performance of the fused results by flow matching, which progressively refines the fused features by learning the transformation from coarse data to high-quality representations. Extensive experiments conducted on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that FMRFusion achieves superior and consistent performance across a range of fusion tasks, especially in nighttime scenarios

</details>


### [51] [Learning a Semantic Calibration Network for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08001)
*Yang Sun,Tao Wang,Anastasia Ioannou,Ge Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义校准网络(SCN)，通过类消歧(CD)和对数融合(LF)两个核心模块，显式建模类别间语义相关性来优化掩码分类过程，在提升模型判别能力的同时保持CLIP的泛化性能，在多个主流基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇语义分割方法要么聚焦特征聚合，要么简单微调预训练模型，未能有效建模类别间的语义相关性，导致模型判别能力不足且容易破坏预训练CLIP模型的泛化能力。

Method: SCN包含两个核心组件：(1) 类消歧模块(CD)：通过交叉注意力将文本嵌入转换为视觉感知的伪文本嵌入，生成增强相似度评分；再利用残差架构捕获隐式类间依赖关系以解决语义模糊性。(2) 对数融合模块(LF)：动态整合多层面语义证据，确保模型在保持CLIP泛化能力的同时达成鲁棒的语义共识。

Result: 在多个主流基准上的综合实验结果表明，所提出的方法相比当前最先进算法取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模类别间语义相关性并动态融合多层面语义证据，SCN在开放词汇语义分割任务上兼顾了判别能力提升与预训练模型泛化能力保持，验证了该思路的有效性。

Abstract: Semantic image segmentation assigns a predefined category label to each pixel, has achieved significant progress lately. Open-Vocabulary Segmentation (OVS) extends the segmentation task from a fixed set to an open set, enabling the identification and segmentation of novel concepts based on arbitrary text inputs, such as category names or descriptions. In this paper, we propose a novel Semantic Calibration Network (SCN) for open-vocabulary semantic segmentation. Different from prior approaches that focus on feature aggregation or simple fine-tuning of pre-trained models, SCN refines the mask classification process by explicitly modeling the semantic correlations between classes, aiming to enhance the model's discriminative power while effectively preserving the generalization abilities of the pre-trained CLIP model. Specifically, SCN comprises two core components: Class Disambiguation (CD) and Logits Fusion (LF). First, a cross-attention mechanism is utilized to transform the text embeddings into visually aware pseudo-text embeddings, in order to derive an enhanced similarity score that complements the original mask-text similarity score. Subsequently, the Class Disambiguation module captures implicit inter-class dependencies through a residual architecture to effectively resolve semantic ambiguities. Finally, the Logits Fusion module dynamically integrates multifaceted semantic evidence to ensure that the model achieves a robust semantic consensus while maintaining CLIP's inherent generalization capability. Comprehensive experimental results on mainstream benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves significant performance improvements compared to state-of-the-art algorithms.

</details>


### [52] [Aqua Boundary-Saliency Attention Module for Lightweight Underwater Salient Instance Segmentation Detection Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08002)
*M. Fazri Nizar,Julian Supardi,Muhammad Naufal Rachmatullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LUSIS-DETR，一种轻量级水下显著实例分割检测Transformer框架，通过AquaBSAM模块嵌入水下先验知识，在四个数据集上达到领先性能，并实现实时推理（T4 GPU上4.31-6.34ms）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示或辅助模态的方法依赖大型基础模型、提示生成或额外模态估计，难以高效部署。亟需一种轻量级、无需额外模态的高效水下实例分割方法。

Method: 提出LUSIS-DETR框架，核心是AquaBSAM模块，将水下边界、对比度、衰减、色度、暗通道和中心先验嵌入DINOv2初始化的多尺度特征中，采用有界残差调制；训练阶段引入辅助掩码监督和小目标复制-粘贴增强。

Result: 在UIIS、UIIS10K、USIS10K、USIS16K四个数据集上，在类别感知和显著实例协议下均达到领先性能；TensorRT FP16在T4 GPU上延迟仅4.31-6.34ms，支持实时推理。

Conclusion: LUSIS-DETR在保持轻量高效的同时实现了竞争性领先的分割性能，且无需大模型或额外模态，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Underwater instance segmentation integrates pixel-level mask prediction and instance-level discrimination for marine resource exploration, ecological monitoring, and underwater robotic perception. Recent prompt-based and auxiliary-modality methods improve mask quality, but their reliance on large foundation models, prompt generation, or extra modality estimation complicates efficient deployment. This work introduces Lightweight Underwater Salient Instance Segmentation Detection Transformer (LUSIS-DETR), a compact detection-transformer framework built around the Aqua Boundary-Saliency Attention Module (AquaBSAM). AquaBSAM embeds underwater boundary, contrast, attenuation, chroma, dark-channel, and center-prior cues into DINOv2-initialized multi-scale features through bounded residual modulation, while auxiliary mask supervision and small-object copy-paste are training-only. Extensive evaluation on four recent underwater instance segmentation datasets, UIIS, UIIS10K, USIS10K, and USIS16K, shows competitively leading performance against previous state-of-the-art works across category-aware and salient-instance protocols. TensorRT half-precision (FP16) benchmarking on an NVIDIA T4 graphics processing unit (GPU) achieves 4.31-6.34 milliseconds (ms) latency, supporting real-time inference under an accessible reproduction setting.

</details>


### [53] [GVC-Seg: Training-Free 3D Instance Segmentation via Geometric Visual Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08014)
*Liang Xu,Fangjing Wang,Jinyu Yang,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的3D实例分割方法GVC-Seg，通过几何视觉对应来缓解不同预训练模型间的置信度偏差，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多预训练基础模型的3D实例分割方法由于不同分割模型的置信度水平固有差异，会产生模型依赖的偏差（偏向高置信度模型），导致次优结果。

Method: 提出GVC-Seg：1）利用3D几何线索与2D视觉线索之间的对应关系（Geometric Visual Correspondence）来缓解置信度偏差；2）引入3D提议生成模块用于实例掩码生成；3）引入掩码感知的CLIP特征提取模块用于实例语义推理；整体为无训练（training-free）方法。

Result: 在多个挑战性基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，并在开放词汇语义分割场景中展现出强潜力。

Conclusion: GVC-Seg通过无训练的几何-视觉对应机制有效缓解了多模型集成中的置信度偏差问题，提升了3D实例分割性能，并具备良好的开放词汇泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate 3D instance segmentation in point cloud data is critical for machine vision applications. Recent advancements leverage multiple pre-trained foundation models to generate 3D proposals, followed by the application of proposal aggregation methods, which significantly enhance performance. However, they often produce sub-optimal results due to inherent variations in confidence levels across different segmentation models, resulting in a bias toward the model with higher confidence. This bias is inherently model-dependent and is influenced by factors such as data preprocessing techniques and training strategies. To address this bias, we propose a novel, training-free 3D instance segmentation approach via Geometric Visual Correspondence (GVC-Seg), which exploits the correspondence between 3D geometric cues and 2D visual cues to mitigate the confidence bias. Additionally, a 3D proposal generation module and a mask-aware CLIP feature extraction module are introduced during the instance mask generation and instance semantic reasoning, respectively. In this way, GVC-Seg enhances proposal quality assessment, ensuring unbiased ensemble learning across different models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on several challenging benchmarks, while also exhibiting strong potential in open-vocabulary semantic segmentation settings.

</details>


### [54] [IEA: Amateur-Friendly Conversational Image Editing Agent via Three Stages of Multitask Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08016)
*Zichen Zhu,Yuheng Sun,Mingxuan Zhu,Wenjie Ma,Situo Zhang,Zhexiang Wang,Ziyue Yang,Danyang Zhang,Kunyao Lan,Zihan Zhao,Dingye Liu,Siqi Xiang,Lu Chen,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种可解释的图像编辑代理IEA，通过三步多任务训练（SFT+GRPO+合成微调）学习操作16种参数化工具，在编辑任务上取得更低像素距离，在摘要任务上取得更高ROUGE-L，用户研究中指令跟随和感知质量均优，验证了可解释、工具中心的VLM是可靠方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑软件依赖固定滤镜或专家调参，导致业余用户意图与结果之间存在差距；生成式模型产生的编辑结果存在伪影、不合理细节或风格漂移，且缺乏可解释性，用户无法了解编辑原因。

Method: 提出IEA（对话式图像编辑代理），三步多任务训练流水线：(1) 在蒸馏专家编辑数据上进行SFT；(2) GRPO强化学习，奖励包括图像相似度改进、工具有用性和意图摘要质量；(3) 大规模合成微调，联合学习图像编辑、优化和用户意图摘要。最终通过操作16种编辑工具逐步生成可检查调试的透明编辑轨迹。

Result: 编辑任务上像素距离低于强基线；摘要任务上ROUGE-L高于强基线；用户研究中，在工具调用方法中指令跟随最佳，整体感知质量超过生成式方法。

Conclusion: 可解释、工具中心的视觉语言模型是实现人类指令引导图像修图的可靠路径。

Abstract: Current image editing software often hinges on fixed filters or expert tuning, leaving a gap between amateur users' intent and outcomes. Creations by generative models may contain artifacts, implausible details, or stylistic drift away from photorealism and offer little insight into why an edit was made. We propose IEA, a conversational Image Editing Agent that learns to operate parameterized tools in an explicit, interpretable action space. IEA is trained via a three-stage multitask pipeline: (1) SFT on distilled expert edits, (2) GRPO with rewards for likeness improvement, tool usefulness, and intent summarization, and (3) large-scale synthetic fine-tuning to jointly master image editing, refinement, and user intent summarization. By manipulating 16 editing tools step by step, IEA produces transparent edit traces that can be inspected and debugged. In quantitative experiments, it attains a lower pixel distance on the edit task and a higher ROUGE-L on the summary task than strong baselines. In user studies, it ranks best among tool-calling methods for instruction following while surpassing generative methods in overall perceptual quality. Our results validate interpretable, tool-centric VLMs as a reliable path to human instruction-guided image retouching.

</details>


### [55] [Vision-Language Asymmetry in Bistable Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08031)
*Arohan Agate*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过构建3,320次生成的行为基线、训练TopK稀疏自编码器分析CLIP层表征，以及进行因果干预实验，揭示了视觉-语言模型在处理模糊图像时（如维特根斯坦的鸭-兔图）的“主导性瓶颈”位于视觉塔下游，而非视觉表征层；同时发现视觉塔中可同时激活两个维度的特征池（叠加态），但语言侧的承诺（commitment）仅选择其一，从而为“看见/看见-as”的区分提供了经验性抓手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维特根斯坦的鸭-兔图提出了一个关键问题：当视觉-语言模型（VLM）对模糊（bistable）图像生成描述时，模型在何处做出了“承诺”（即选择某一方面的解释）？现有研究缺乏对VLM内部表征与输出行为之间关系的系统性分析。

Method: 1) 行为基线：在83个双稳态刺激上收集3,320次生成，区分三种行为模式（默认主导、强制主导、强制平衡），并在中性提示与强制选择提示下对比。2) 表征探针：在LLaVA-1.6-7B实际消费的CLIP层上训练TopK稀疏自编码器（验证EV 0.93），提取每个“方面”（aspect）对应的特征池（feature pool）。3) 因果干预：在CLIP第22层进行因果转向（causal steering），测试对模型输出的影响。

Result: 1) 在69个双稳态刺激中，72%（50/69）在视觉塔中同时激活了两个方面的特征池（包括12/12的默认主导鸭/兔刺激，以及7/8的强制平衡年轻/老年刺激）。2) 因果转向能在默认主导刺激上翻转描述（鸭→兔翻转率33%，受流畅性约束），但对强制平衡的年轻/老年刺激，在任何测试系数下均无法翻转描述。3) 视觉侧表征的“叠加”与语言侧的“唯一承诺”之间存在差距。4) 方法论发现：对TopK SAE输出使用基于秩的统计时，需要修正秩中的并列关系（tie-corrected ranking），否则会产生静默的行序偏差。

Conclusion: 主导性瓶颈（dominance bottleneck）位于视觉塔下游，而非视觉表征层本身。视觉-语言模型中“视觉侧的表征叠加”与“语言侧的单一承诺”之间的差距，为理解“看见”（seeing）与“看见-as”（seeing-as）的区别提供了一个经验性的分析抓手。

Abstract: Wittgenstein's duck-rabbit poses a question for vision-language models: when a model captions an ambiguous image, where in the model is the commitment to one aspect made? We address this with a 3,320-generation behavioral baseline over 83 bistable stimuli that surfaces three regimes (default-dominant, force-dominant, force-balanced) under neutral vs forced-choice prompting, then probe the underlying representations using a TopK sparse autoencoder we train on the CLIP layer that LLaVA-1.6-7B actually consumes (validation EV 0.93). Across 69 bistable stimuli with both per-aspect feature pools available, 72% (50/69) show simultaneous activation of both pools at the vision tower, including 12/12 default-dominant duck/rabbit and 7/8 force-balanced young/old. Causal steering at CLIP layer 22 flips captions on default-dominant stimuli (33% rabbit-flip rate under a fluency guard) but cannot flip captions on force-balanced young/old at any tested coefficient, despite their vision-side superposition. The dominance bottleneck lives downstream of the vision tower; the gap between vision-side representation and language-side commitment is an empirical handle on the seeing/seeing-as distinction. We also flag a methodological note: rank-based statistics on TopK SAE outputs require tie-corrected ranking to avoid silent row-order bias.

</details>


### [56] [Balancing Real and Synthetic Data for CNN-based Masonry Crack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08033)
*Mattia Forlesi,Alfonso Esposito,Ivan Zyrianoff,Alessandro Marzani,Marco Di Felice*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了使用合成裂纹数据补充真实数据来训练深度学习模型进行 masonry 裂缝检测。实验表明，20%真实数据+80%合成数据的训练方案在F1-score (76%)和mIoU (80%)上甚至超越了纯真实数据训练的效果，证明了合成数据在减少数据收集成本的同时提升检测精度的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN性能高度依赖大规模多样化数据集，但 masonry 等复杂表面的真实裂纹数据收集耗时费力，公开数据集也未必适用，因此需要探索合成数据来弥补真实数据不足的问题。

Method: 使用 crack overlay 工具在背景图像上按受控方向和位置添加裂纹生成合成数据集；用真实数据训练多种DL架构选出最佳模型 InceptionV4；设置六种不同比例的真实/合成数据训练场景，在真实图像测试集上评估F1-score和mIoU。

Result: 合成数据+20%真实数据即可达到与纯真实数据训练相当的效果；20/80（合成/真实）场景达到76% F1-score和80% mIoU，优于纯真实数据训练。

Conclusion: 合成数据有潜力减少数据收集工作量，同时提升裂缝检测精度。

Abstract: Cracks are a critical indicator of building health, and early stage identification is fundamental to prevent harmful damages. Advances in deep learning (DL), particularly convolutional neural networks (CNNs), have enabled scalable solutions for automated crack detection. However, CNN performance strongly depends on the availability of large and diverse datasets, which is particularly challenging for complex surfaces such as masonry. Collecting sufficient real data is time-consuming, while publicly available datasets may not be adequate. To address this limitation, we explored generating synthetic crack data, which complements real data and improves training effectiveness. The real dataset consists of masonry crack images collected from buildings in Bologna and surrounding areas. In contrast, the synthetic dataset was generated using a crack overlay tool that adds cracks to background images in a controlled orientation and placement. The real dataset was used to train several DL architectures, to identify the best-performing model (InceptionV4) employed for experiments with generated data. Six training scenarios were tested in InceptionV4 by varying the ratio of real and synthetic data, with evaluation performed on a test set composed of real images using the F1-score and mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) metrics. Results show that training on synthetic data plus a modest addition of 20% real data achieves results comparable to training on real data only. Moreover, the 20/80 scenario (synthetic/real) achieved an 76% F1-score and 80% mean IoU, outperforming the real-only case. As can be seen, the method demonstrates the potential of synthetic data to reduce collection efforts while enhancing crack detection accuracy.

</details>


### [57] [Sci-Rho: A Multilingual Visually-Grounded Symbolic Benchmark for STEM Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08034)
*Muhammad Falensi Azmi,Ikhlasul Akmal Hanif,Vallerie Alexandra Putra,Adi Yeltay,Abdullah Mubarak,Fajri Koto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Sci-Rho，一个多语言、多学科、视觉化的STEM动态基准测试，包含4,242个模板和42,420个实例，用于评估视觉语言模型在问题变体下的鲁棒性。实验发现，模型在最差情况准确率与平均准确率之间存在显著差距，且较小模型在跨语言场景下性能下降明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号基准大多局限于数学推理、缺乏视觉基础且以英语为主，无法全面评估模型在STEM领域不同语言和视觉变体下的鲁棒性。

Method: 设计Sci-Rho基准，涵盖5个学科、7种语言，由领域专家（含奥赛奖牌得主）构建4,242个问题模板，每个模板以可执行Python代码实现，通过变化数值、视觉模式、几何形状、配色方案和函数类型生成多样但等价的实例（共42,420个），并评估17个最先进的VLM。

Result: 模型在最差情况准确率与平均准确率之间存在显著差距；较小模型在跨语言场景下性能退化明显，而专有模型和大型模型保持鲁棒；注意力头分析显示不同语言间图像token与文本token的相对注意力分配存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 评估不应局限于静态基准，动态多语言视觉基准对于衡量VLM质量至关重要。

Abstract: Symbolic benchmarks have emerged as a key approach to assess model robustness under minor modifications to STEM-related questions. However, existing symbolic benchmarks mostly remain limited to mathematical reasoning, lack visual grounding, and are predominantly in English. In this work, we introduce Sci-Rho (Science Rhobustness), a dynamic benchmark for visually-grounded STEM problems spanning five subjects and seven languages, comprising 4,242 problem templates (606 per language) crafted by domain experts, including Olympiad medalists. Each template is implemented as executable Python code that generates diverse but equivalent problem instances by varying numerical values, visual patterns, geometric shapes, color schemes, and function types, resulting in 42,420 instances in total, each paired with reasoning steps and ground-truth solutions. We evaluated 17 state-of-the-art VLMs and discovered a noticeable gap between worst-case accuracy (defined as the proportion of problem templates that a model answers correctly across every generated variation) and average accuracy. We also discovered that smaller models show noticeable performance degradation across languages, whereas proprietary and larger models remain robust. Step-level evaluation reflects this same trend, revealing a significant gap between average F1 and worst-case F1 scores. Finally, our inspection of attention heads of a VLM reveals substantial cross-lingual variation in the relative attention allocated to image tokens compared to text tokens. Our work highlights the importance of evaluation beyond static benchmarks as a metric to measure the quality of VLMs.

</details>


### [58] [DyCo-RL: Dynamic Cross-Modal Coordination for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08035)
*Hangui Lin,Yan Shu,Zhengyang Liang,Chi Liu,Xiangrui Liu,Minghao Qin,Teng Long,Zheng Liu,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DyCo-RL，通过Fisher-Rao测度量化跨模态协调，对RLVR中忽视的推理过程动态协调问题进行优化，在7个基准上一致提升4种RLVR算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅优化推理结果，忽视了MLLM在CoT推理过程中提取视觉证据与合成文本上下文之间的动态跨模态协调问题，而这种协调失败是推理错误的根本原因。

Method: 利用Fisher-Rao测地距离测量模态内注意力偏移，将token分为视觉导向或文本导向功能角色，然后评估token实际注意力分配与分配角色的对齐程度，基于此得分进行对齐引导的优势重新加权。

Result: DyCo-RL在Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B上，对4种代表性RLVR算法在7个视觉和数学推理基准上均实现一致改进。

Conclusion: DyCo-RL是一种算法无关的增强方法，通过将动态跨模态协调纳入RLVR优化，有效提升了多模态大语言模型的视觉推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a leading paradigm for enhancing visual reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, existing RLVR methods optimize primarily for the reasoning outcome, fundamentally overlooking the fine-grained cross-modal coordination required during the generation process. Through token-level analyses and controlled interventions, we reveal that during Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, MLLMs frequently fail to dynamically alternate between extracting visual evidence and synthesizing textual context-a coordination breakdown that is causally linked to reasoning failures. Motivated by these findings, we propose DyCo-RL, which integrates dynamic cross-modal coordination into RLVR optimization. Specifically, DyCo-RL uses the Fisher-Rao geodesic distance to measure within-modality attention shifts, assigning tokens to either visually-oriented or text-oriented functional roles. It then evaluates the alignment between a token's actual attention allocation and its assigned role, leveraging this score for alignment-guided advantage reweighting during policy optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the algorithm-agnostic DyCo-RL, when applied to Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B, consistently improves four representative RLVR algorithms across seven benchmarks spanning visual-centric and mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [59] [Robust-U1: Can MLLMs Self-Recover Corrupted Visual Content for Robust Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08063)
*Jiaqi Tang,Jianmin Chen,Youyang Zhai,Wei Wei,Runtao Liu,Mengjie Zhao,Xiangyu Wu,Qingfa Xiao,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Robust-U1框架，赋予多模态大语言模型显式的视觉自恢复能力，通过监督微调初始重建、强化学习双奖励对齐高质量视觉、以及多模态推理联合处理受损和恢复图像，在真实世界和对抗性视觉损坏场景下达到最先进的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在真实视觉损坏下性能显著下降，而现有鲁棒增强方法（黑盒特征对齐缺乏可解释性，白盒文本推理无法恢复像素级细节）均存在局限。本文探索MLLMs能否自行恢复受损视觉内容这一基础研究问题。

Method: 提出Robust-U1框架，包含三阶段：1）监督微调进行初始重建；2）强化学习，使用像素级SSIM和语义级CLIP相似度双奖励对齐高质量视觉；3）多模态推理，联合考虑损坏输入和恢复图像进行推理。

Result: 在真实世界损坏基准上达到最先进鲁棒性，在通用VQA基准的对抗性损坏下保持优越性能。高质量视觉恢复直接增强推理性能。

Conclusion: 自恢复机制是鲁棒视觉理解的关键机制，证明MLLMs具备自我恢复受损视觉内容的能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in visual understanding, yet their performance degrades significantly under real-world visual corruptions. While existing robustness enhancement approaches exist, they are limited: black-box feature alignment lacks interpretability, and white-box text-based reasoning cannot restore lost pixel-level details. This work investigates a fundamental research question: Can MLLMs recover corrupted visual content by themselves? To address this, we propose Robust-U1, a novel framework that equips MLLMs with explicit visual self-recovery capability for robust understanding. The approach comprises three core stages: supervised fine-tuning for initial reconstruction, reinforcement learning with dual rewards (pixel-level SSIM and semantic-level CLIP similarity) for aligning high visual quality, and multimodal reasoning that jointly considers both the corrupted input and the recovered image. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Robust-U1 achieves state-of-the-art robustness on the real-world corruption benchmark and maintains superior performance under adversarial corruptions on general VQA benchmarks. Analysis confirms that high-quality visual recovery directly enhances reasoning performance, establishing self-recovery as a critical mechanism for robust visual understanding. The source code is available at https://github.com/jqtangust/Robust-U1.

</details>


### [60] [VideoWeaver: Evaluating and Evolving Skills for Agentic Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08091)
*Jianhui Wei,Jie Tan,Hengchuan Zhu,Xiaotian Zhang,Yan Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Daoan Zhang,Wei Xu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoWeaver是一个面向长视频生成任务的智能体框架与评估基准，通过技能组合与进化机制，让智能体自主构建工作流而非遵循固定管线，并提出了基于执行轨迹与最终视频的智能体评判方法。实验表明显式组合技能、技能进化算法能有效提升生成质量，智能体评判与人类判断高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架（如Claude Code、Codex、OpenClaw）在工具使用和编排方面表现强劲，但它们在长视频生成这一长时程多模态任务上的能力尚未得到充分探索。不同于早期手工设计管线的视频智能体，这些框架能够构建和优化自身工作流，因此需要一个评估和演化其长视频生成技能的基准与平台。

Method: 1) 构建VideoWeaver智能体框架，让智能体通过组合基础技能自主生成工作流而非遵循预定义管线；2) 建立包含16个任务类别、285个案例的基准数据集，参照涵盖文本、图像、音频、视频及其组合；3) 提出agent-as-judge评判方法，通过检查执行轨迹和最终视频，基于元数据、中间文件等证据进行评分；4) 设计技能进化算法，利用评判反馈优化和合并智能体技能。

Result: 在多个框架和模型上的实验表明：显式组合技能相比仅使用基础技能能改善生成过程；技能进化算法能进一步提升输出质量；不同框架和模型选择下性能差异显著；所提出的agent-as-judge与人类判断高度一致，特别是在过程指标上。

Conclusion: VideoWeaver为长视频生成任务提供了有效的智能体评估与技能进化平台，证明了自主工作流构建和技能进化的价值，且智能体评判方法可作为人类评估的可靠替代方案。

Abstract: Recent agent frameworks such as Claude Code, Codex, and OpenClaw are strong at tool use and orchestration, but whether they can handle long video generation, a long-horizon multimodal task, remains underexplored. Unlike earlier video agents whose pipeline is handcrafted, these frameworks can build and refine their own workflows. We introduce VideoWeaver, an agent harness and benchmark that evaluates and evolves skills for long video generation, where an agent turns a single instruction into a long video by composing foundation skills into its own workflow rather than following a predefined pipeline. The benchmark has 16 task categories and 285 cases, with references spanning text, image, audio, video, and their combinations. Because errors can arise at any stage and not just in the final video, we propose an agent-as-judge that inspects both the execution trace and the final video, grounding its scores in evidence such as metadata and intermediate files. Using this feedback, we further design a skill evolution algorithm that refines and merges the agent's skills. Across multiple frameworks and models, we find that an explicit composition skill improves the generation process over using foundation skills alone, that skill evolution further improves output quality, and that performance varies notably across harness and model choices. The proposed agent-as-judge also aligns well with human judgments, especially on process metrics. Code and dataset is available at https://github.com/JianhuiWei7/VideoWeaver

</details>


### [61] [Trustworthy Visual Predicates for Robust Manipulation Understanding under Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08121)
*Fatemeh Ziaeetabar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种谓词级可靠性框架，用于评估视觉退化（模糊、遮挡、光照变化、低分辨率、丢帧、检测噪声）下操作理解中各类谓词（接触、支撑、包含、运动耦合、抓取、释放、主动手部参与）的可靠性。实验表明，静态空间谓词较鲁棒，而接触敏感、动态和派生谓词（如抓取、释放）更脆弱；严重退化下检测噪声、遮挡和丢帧导致最强可靠性损失；下游操作理解准确率从0.89降至0.58。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作理解需要可靠的视觉关系证据（如接触、支撑、包含等谓词），但这些谓词在视觉退化条件下的可靠性缺乏直接分析，现有研究多聚焦于事件链、图模型和神经符号模型的应用，而忽略了谓词级可靠性的系统性评估。

Method: 提出谓词级可靠性框架，包括：结构化谓词词汇表、置信度感知的谓词估计、以及评估谓词保持性、退化敏感性、时间一致性、置信加权稳定性和下游影响的可靠性指标。在受控操作视频和公开数据集（VISOR/EPIC-KITCHENS、H2O、ARCTIC）上进行实验。

Result: 谓词失败是结构化的而非均匀的：静态空间谓词较鲁棒，接触敏感、动态和派生谓词（抓取、释放）更脆弱。严重退化下检测噪声、遮挡和丢帧导致最强可靠性损失。下游分析显示退化谓词使操作理解准确率从0.89降至0.58；中度退化下去除置信加权使准确率从0.74降至0.64。

Conclusion: 谓词可靠性提供了一个介于视觉感知和结构化操作推理之间的诊断层，有助于理解视觉退化对操作理解的影响机制。

Abstract: Manipulation understanding requires reliable relational evidence, such as contact, support, containment, motion coupling, grasp, release, and active-hand involvement. Although these visual predicates are widely used in event-chain, graph-based, and neuro-symbolic models, their reliability under visual degradation is rarely analyzed directly. This paper introduces a predicate-level reliability framework for robust manipulation understanding under blur, occlusion, illumination change, low resolution, frame dropping, and detection noise. The framework defines a structured predicate vocabulary, confidence-aware predicate estimation, and reliability metrics for predicate preservation, degradation sensitivity, temporal consistency, confidence-weighted stability, and downstream impact. Experiments on controlled manipulation videos and public egocentric or bimanual datasets, including VISOR/EPIC-KITCHENS, H2O, and ARCTIC, show that predicate failures are structured rather than uniform. Static spatial predicates remain comparatively robust, whereas contact-sensitive, dynamic, and derived predicates such as grasp and release are more fragile. Under severe degradation, detection noise, occlusion, and frame dropping cause the strongest reliability losses. Downstream analysis shows that degraded predicates reduce manipulation-understanding accuracy from 0.89 to 0.58, while removing confidence weighting under moderate degradation reduces accuracy from 0.74 to 0.64. These results show that predicate reliability provides a diagnostic layer between visual perception and structured manipulation reasoning.

</details>


### [62] [Human-Centered Benchmarking of Driver Monitoring Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08123)
*Ruben Dario Florez-Zela*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出人类中心基准框架（HCBF），从准确率、可解释性、效率和鲁棒性四个维度评估驾驶员监控模型。在MRL眼部数据集上对四种轻量级架构测试发现：传统准确率指标无法区分模型性能，各模型在不同维度各有优劣，而综合排名第一的ShuffleNetV2在传感器噪声下表现脆弱，Transformer虽综合排名靠后但鲁棒性最强。这表明多维度、人类中心的评估对于实际部署至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉的驾驶员监控系统评估几乎仅依赖分类准确率，但准确率不足以刻画模型在真实场景中的部署适应性，亟需一种更全面、以人为中心的评估框架。

Method: 提出HCBF框架，涵盖四个评估维度：准确率、可解释性、效率和鲁棒性。在MRL眼部数据集上对MobileNetV3、ShuffleNetV2、EfficientNet-B0和DeiT-Tiny四种轻量级架构进行眼状态分类评估，并设计三种部署场景权重计算人类中心综合得分。

Result: 四种模型在干净测试集上准确率几乎无差别，但各在一个维度上表现最优且均处于帕累托前沿；综合得分ShuffleNetV2在所有场景下排名第一，但其在传感器噪声下性能保留不足一半，且将闭眼状态误分类为睁眼；Transformer模型虽综合排名靠后但鲁棒性最强。

Conclusion: 综合排名可能掩盖维度特定的脆弱性，而这些脆弱性在实际运营中可能是决定性的。多维度、人类中心的评估框架比单一准确率指标更能揭示模型的真实部署适应性。

Abstract: Vision-based driver monitoring systems are increasingly deployed in safety-critical intelligent transportation settings, yet they are almost always compared on classification accuracy alone. This paper argues that accuracy is insufficient to characterize a model's fitness for real-world deployment, and proposes the Human-Centered Benchmarking Framework (HCBF), which evaluates models across four dimensions: accuracy, explainability, efficiency, and robustness. The framework is applied to four representative lightweight architectures, MobileNetV3, ShuffleNetV2, EfficientNet-B0, and DeiT-Tiny, on the MRL Eye Dataset for eye-state classification. While the models are nearly indistinguishable on clean-set accuracy, each leads in exactly one dimension, and all four lie on the Pareto frontier. A Human-Centered Score computed under three deployment-oriented weighting scenarios ranks ShuffleNetV2 first throughout. However, this aggregate winner retains less than half of its performance under sensor noise and fails by classifying closed eyes as open, whereas the transformer remains robust. These findings show that aggregate ranking can mask dimension-specific vulnerabilities that are operationally decisive, underscoring the value of multi-dimensional, human-centered evaluation.

</details>


### [63] [One Stone, Three Birds: Self-adaptive Optimal Transport for Multi-VLM Selection, Adaptation, and Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08126)
*Qiyu Xu,Zhanxuan Hu,Yu Duan,Yonghang Tai,Huafeng Li,Quanxue Gao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OSTB框架，通过自适应最优传输在无目标标签条件下同时实现多VLM的模型选择、目标适配和预测集成三大任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单骨干VLM部署范式隐含假设所选VLM与目标域兼容，但在跨域部署中多个通用和领域专用VLM候选皆有可能，缺乏实例级标签无法识别可靠模型，需要同时解决模型选择、目标适配和预测集成三个耦合问题。

Method: 提出One Stone, Three Birds (OSTB)训练无关框架，基于自适应最优传输。在冻结候选VLM池上估计共识样本-类别传输计划，随后复用该结构：通过语义与视觉可靠性排序进行模型选择；拟合传输条件化的视觉分类器实现目标适配；通过可靠性感知概率集成实现预测融合。

Result: 在自然图像、遥感图像和医学病理图像基准上，OSTB在异质候选池下提升了模型排序质量、适配稳定性和集成鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 证明了多VLM系统级视角下，通过最优传输估计共识样本-类别结构可统一解决模型选择、适配与集成三个耦合问题，无需更新模型参数或依赖目标标签。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable visual recognition from semantic class descriptions, which makes them attractive when target annotations are scarce or unavailable. Most deployment pipelines, however, first choose a single VLM and then adapt that model to the unlabeled target set. This single-backbone paradigm hides a critical assumption: the selected VLM is already compatible with the target domain. In realistic cross-domain deployment, several general-purpose and domain-specialized VLMs may be plausible, yet no instance-level target labels are available to identify the reliable ones. Deployment therefore requires a coupled solution for model selection, target adaptation, and prediction integration. We revisit this problem from a system-level multi-VLM perspective. Our central observation is that the three decisions above depend on the same latent object: a trustworthy sample-class structure in the target set. Different VLMs may encode different transfer biases and produce conflicting predictions, but their outputs can still provide complementary evidence for estimating this structure. We propose One Stone, Three Birds, a training-free framework based on self-adaptive optimal transport. Given a pool of frozen candidate VLMs, OSTB estimates a consensus sample-to-class transport plan without updating VLM parameters. The learned transport structure is then reused for all deployment objectives: model selection is performed by ranking the combined semantic and visual reliability induced by the consensus plan; target adaptation is obtained by fitting transport-conditioned visual classifiers; and ensembling is implemented through reliability-aware probabilistic integration. Extensive experiments on natural-image, remote-sensing, and medical-pathology benchmarks show that OSTB improves model ranking, adaptation stability, and ensemble robustness under heterogeneous candidate pools.

</details>


### [64] [Phase Marginalization for Patch-Grid Instability in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08132)
*Oğuzhan Ercan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出补丁网格相位作为视觉Transformer密集预测中的 nuisance 变量，并设计了无需训练的相位边缘化方法（K=4），通过在多个补丁相位上对齐聚合结果，在不增加训练成本的情况下提升分割、深度估计和局部匹配的性能，在Cityscapes上比四倍测试时增强（TTA）有0.31 mIoU的提升，且K=4是精度与延迟的最佳折衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer在密集预测任务中因固定的补丁网格划分引入相位依赖的不稳定性——改变补丁划分会改变像素可用的token证据，尤其边界附近。作者将这一现象形式化为 nuisance 变量，旨在提出一种后处理方法消除该影响。

Method: 提出Phase Marginalization后验边缘化方法：评估结构化的补丁网格相位，对每个相位的输出进行逆对齐，然后在原始图像坐标系中聚合结果。核心变体Uniform Phase Marginalization with K=4无需训练，通过枚举4种均匀相位偏移并聚合来边缘化相位影响。

Result: 在Cityscapes上，K=4相比标准K=1基线在分割、深度估计和局部匹配上均有提升；相比四倍测试时增强（TTA）优势为+0.31 mIoU。缩放实验显示K=4是最优性价比选择，K=8基本不变，K=16精度提升微弱但延迟显著增加。

Conclusion: 补丁网格相位是可测量的 nuisance 变量，Phase Marginalization可作为密集ViT预测的简单诊断和基线方法。

Abstract: Vision Transformers operate on fixed patch grids, which can introduce phase-dependent instability for dense prediction: changing the patch partition can change the token evidence available to a pixel, especially near boundaries. We formalize patch-grid phase as a nuisance variable and propose Phase Marginalization, a post-hoc marginalization method that evaluates structured patch-grid phases, inverse-aligns dense outputs, and aggregates them in the original image coordinate system. The central variant, Uniform Phase Marginalization with K = 4, is training-free and improves over the canonical K = 1 baseline across measured segmentation, depth, and local matching settings. In a controlled Cityscapes experiment, Uniform Phase Marginalization provides a modest compute-matched advantage over generic shift-based four-forward test-time augmentation (TTA) (+0.31 mean Intersection-over-Union over the strongest tested generic row). A scaling study further shows that K = 4 is a practical cost-accuracy trade-off: K = 8 is essentially unchanged and K = 16 adds little accuracy at much higher latency. These results position patch-grid phase as a measurable nuisance variable and Phase Marginalization as a simple diagnostic and post-hoc marginalization baseline for dense ViT prediction.

</details>


### [65] [Gravity-guided Contact Dynamics Estimation from 3D Human Motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08133)
*Cuong Le,Urs Waldmann,Bastian Wandt,Mårten Wadenbäck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GraCE模型，利用人体重心和重力引导，从运动捕捉数据中估计全身地面接触动力学，包括接触力与压力分布，在GroundLink和MOYO数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖测力台或压力垫，限制于受控环境；而基于运动捕捉的估计方法仅用垂直距离粗略估计，无法捕捉复杂压力分布。

Method: 利用人体重心与身体的相对距离估计地面接触，通过预测的接触概率与由质心轨迹计算的总外力之积来估计每个接触点上的作用力。

Result: 在GroundLink数据集上地面反作用力估计优于相关工作，在MOYO数据集上接触压力预测也取得更好结果。

Conclusion: GraCE通过引入重心与重力引导，实现了更准确、更细粒度的全身地面接触动力学估计，具备更好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Ground contact forces acting on the human body, are crucial for biomechanics studies or sport performance analysis. Prior methods rely on force plates or pressure mats to collect ground contact dynamics, limiting their applicability to carefully controlled settings. A more scalable solution is to estimate the dynamics directly from motion capture data. Recent approaches only roughly estimate the ground contact dynamics from the vertical distance between the body and the ground plane, which cannot capture the complex pressure distribution of all contact points. To this end, we propose GraCE -- Gravity-guided Contact Dynamics Estimation, a novel full-body contact dynamics model for human motions using a realistic influence of body mass distribution and gravity. We use the human's center of gravity to estimate the ground contacts based on its relative distance to the human body. The applied force on each contact is estimated via the product of predicted contact probabilities and the total exterior force computed from the center of mass trajectory. We outperform related work on the GroundLink dataset for ground reaction force estimation, and on the MOYO dataset for detailed contact pressure prediction. The code is published upon acceptance.

</details>


### [66] [IMAGINE: Adaptive Schema-Imagery Enhanced Composition for Composed Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08144)
*Jiale Huang,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Chunxiao Wang,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IMAGINE网络，通过动态多模态原型将修改文本中的隐式语义（如"蛋糕"暗示"生日派对"）注入视觉特征，弥合显式内容与隐式意图的差距，在组合视频检索和组合图像检索任务中实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合视频检索方法仅处理显式出现在视频中的对象，忽视了修改文本中隐含的语义关联（如语义相关视觉线索），导致检索意图与视觉内容之间存在语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出IMAGINE网络，通过构建动态多模态原型来捕捉共享的潜在概念（称为图式意象），利用这些原型自适应地调制视觉特征，将隐式语义注入检索过程。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的基准数据集上，IMAGINE在组合视频检索和组合图像检索任务中均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过图式意象机制弥合显式视觉内容与隐式检索意图之间的鸿沟，有效提升了组合检索的语义理解和匹配能力。

Abstract: Composed Video Retrieval (CVR) is designed to retrieve a target video that matches a reference video modified by a modification text. While existing methods explore cross-modal correspondences, they often assume modified objects appear directly in videos. However, modification texts frequently describe concepts not explicitly presented but implicitly expressed through semantically related visual cues (e.g., "cake" implying "birthday party"). Current approaches typically rely on aligning explicit feature representations within the concrete space, neglecting critical latent associations. To address this, we propose an adaptIve scheMa-ImAGery enhanced composItional NEtwork (IMAGINE). Unlike standard explicit matching, IMAGINE materializes implicit semantics (termed schema imagery) via dynamic multimodal prototypes. These prototypes capture shared latent concepts to adaptively modulate visual features, effectively injecting implicit guidance into the retrieval process. By bridging the gap between explicit visual contents and implicit retrieval intentions, IMAGINE achieves state-of-the-art performance in both CVR and Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) across three widely used benchmarks.

</details>


### [67] [Property-Informed Diffusion-Based Text-to-Microstructure Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08150)
*Bingxuan Dai,Hongsong Wang,Jie Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种属性信息驱动的扩散网络（PropDiff-TMG），能够根据文本描述直接生成3D微结构，通过双对齐策略确保生成结构与文本提示的一致性，实验证明该方法能生成语义合理且物理可行的微结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D超材料微结构逆设计方法存在设计多样性不足、生成结构物理可行性难以保证的问题，且通常需要领域专家知识、迭代仿真和大量人工调参。

Method: 提出属性信息驱动的扩散网络，利用文本输入中的语义和物理属性信息进行条件生成，采用双对齐策略（对比性文本-结构对齐和测试时奖励引导对齐）确保生成结构与文本提示的一致性。

Result: 模型能够在多种材料类别上生成语义合理且物理可行的3D微结构，具有良好的交互式设计潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法为结合语言界面的逆材料发现开辟了新方向，有望用于交互式微结构设计。

Abstract: Designing 3D metamaterial microstructures that meet the intended functions remains a major challenge, as it typically requires domain expertise, iterative simulations, and extensive manual tuning. Existing work on inverse design that automatically generates microstructures based on desired target properties often suffers from limited design diversity and faces challenges in ensuring the physical feasibility of the generated structures. To address this issue, a property-informed diffusion-based network is proposed that enables the generation of 3D microstructures directly from textual descriptions. Unlike traditional property conditioning methods, our approach leverages rich guidance in terms of semantics and physical properties in the text input to support diverse structure synthesis. To enforce consistency between the generated structures and the target textual prompts, a dual alignment strategy is adopted, including contrastive text-structure alignment and test-time reward-guided alignment. Experimental results show that the model is capable of generating semantically meaningful and physically plausible structures across a wide range of material categories. Our approach has good potential for interactive microstructure design and opens up new directions for combining language-based interfaces with inverse material discovery. Code is available at: https://github.com/hongsong-wang/PropDiff-TMG

</details>


### [68] [RAPID: Layer-Wise Redundancy-Aware Pruning and Importance-Driven Token Merging for Efficient ViT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08156)
*Kyumin Choi,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAPID提出了一种深度感知的token缩减框架，根据网络层深度自适应调整缩减策略：浅中层采用冗余相似度感知剪枝，深层转为重要性相似度感知合并，在ImageNet-1K上实现了比ToMe和ToFu更优的精度-压缩帕累托前沿，尤其在极端压缩率下比ToMe提升高达4.29%的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers (ViTs) 因二次自注意力复杂度而计算成本高昂；现有token缩减技术（如剪枝和合并）忽略了表征随网络深度的演化特性。

Method: 提出RAPID框架，采用分叉策略：浅到中层使用冗余相似度感知剪枝度量消除过表征的局部模式；深层转为基于CLS token注意力权重的重要性相似度感知合并机制，保护语义关键token同时融合不重要但相似的邻居。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上使用ViT和DeiT架构验证，RAPID在精度-压缩帕累托前沿上优于ToMe和ToFu等基线，在极端缩减率下比ToMe高4.29%精度。

Conclusion: RAPID提供了一种无需训练、即插即用的模板，通过将缩减策略与层级特征演化对齐来优化视觉模型。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance but suffer from high computational costs due to quadratic self-attention complexity. Although token reduction techniques such as pruning and merging mitigate this, they typically overlook how representations evolve across network depth. We propose RAPID, a depth-aware token reduction framework that adapts reduction strategies to the layer-wise characteristics of token representations. The primary methodological contribution is a bifurcated strategy: in shallow-to-middle layers, RAPID employs a redundancy-similarity aware pruning metric to eliminate over-represented local patterns. As features transition to global semantic concepts in deeper layers, the framework shifts to an importance-similarity aware merging mechanism. This stage leverages classification (CLS) token attention weights to protect semantically critical tokens while fusing less important but similar neighbors. Empirical validation on ImageNet-1K using ViT and DeiT architectures demonstrates that RAPID establishes a superior accuracy-compression Pareto frontier compared to plug-and-play baselines such as ToMe and ToFu. RAPID is particularly robust in aggressive compression regimes, achieving up to 4.29% higher accuracy than ToMe at extreme reduction rates. Our framework provides a training-free template for optimizing vision models by aligning reduction strategies with hierarchical feature evolution.

</details>


### [69] [How Much MRI Preprocessing Is Enough? A Cost-Utility Study for Brain MRI Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08164)
*Jiangshuan Pang,Wangyang Tang,Jing Yan,Zhixuan Cheng,Youzhe He,Zhenkun Zhuang,Tao Zhou,Shiping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了3D脑MRI自监督预训练中预处理强度对下游任务的影响，发现预处理并非越强越好：最低可行预处理水平（P2）已能取得接近最优的性能，更强预处理仅在特定任务（如MCI分类）中有可观收益，且其优势可通过仅在下游任务中应用更强预处理来部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI预处理通常被视为常规数据清洗步骤，而非建模选择。然而预处理决定了脑MRI基础模型所见输入的分布，其计算成本与收益之间的关系尚不明确，亟需系统量化评估。

Method: 在固定语料库、3D ViT骨干网络、掩码协议和下游评测的条件下，比较了P0-P7共8个递增预处理级别对MAE和JEPA两种自监督方法的影响。使用20,000个异构脑MRI体积进行预训练，然后在IDH预测、MCI分类、脑年龄回归和GLI/PED肿瘤分割四个下游任务上评估编码器迁移性能。

Result: P0/P1数值不稳定，P2是最低可行级别；在P2以上，选择最优预处理级别对MAE仅提升3.4个百分点，对JEPA仅提升1.8个百分点。IDH任务有适度提升，AGE和GLI/PED在P2附近最优，MCI在P7上有最明显增益。跨级别迁移实验表明MCI的P7优势可通过仅在下游使用更强预处理来恢复。

Conclusion: MRI预处理应被视为下游感知的成本-效用决策，而非默认的升级管线。预处理强度并非越高越好，最低可行水平已能覆盖大部分收益，额外预处理收益有限且具有任务特异性。

Abstract: MRI preprocessing defines the input distribution seen by brain MRI foundation models, yet it is usually treated as routine data cleaning rather than a modeling choice. We ask how much preprocessing is worth its computational cost for self-supervised 3D MRI pretraining. Keeping the corpus, 3D ViT backbone, masking protocol, and downstream evaluations fixed, we compare a graded P0-P7 preprocessing spectrum for masked autoencoding (MAE) and joint-embedding predictive learning (JEPA) on 20,000 heterogeneous brain MRI volumes, then transfer the encoders to IDH prediction, MCI classification, brain age regression, and GLI/PED tumor segmentation. The results do not support a simple "more is better" rule. P0/P1 are numerically unstable, making P2 the lowest-cost feasible level; beyond P2, choosing the best feasible preprocessing level improves aggregate utility by only 3.4 percentage points for MAE and 1.8 percentage points for JEPA, with most paired gains statistically unresolved. Stronger preprocessing is beneficial only in selected regimes: IDH improves modestly, AGE and GLI/PED are often near or best at P2, and MCI shows the clearest empirical P7 gain. Cross-level MCI transfer further shows that much of the P7 advantage can be recovered by applying stronger preprocessing downstream, without requiring P7 throughout pretraining. These findings recast MRI preprocessing as a downstream-aware cost-utility decision rather than a default escalation pipeline. Code is available at https://github.com/PangJiangShuan/PreBrain.

</details>


### [70] [Empowering Feed-Forward Reconstruction Models with Metric Scale via Satellite Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08205)
*Xianghui Ze,Yongjian Luo,Mengjun Chao,Zhenbo Song,Jianfeng Lu,Yujiao Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出卫星引导的框架，通过双向跨视图交互利用卫星图像解决前馈3D重建中的尺度模糊问题，实现公制度量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建模型虽泛化能力强，但只能恢复未知尺度的几何结构（尺度模糊），而现有公制度量方法依赖大规模度量标注或精确相机标定，成本高且不可靠。

Method: 使用卫星图像作为全局度量参考，在给定粗相机位姿后检索局部卫星图像，通过双向跨视图交互与前馈重建骨干集成，强制重建场景与卫星参考之间的一致性，从而推断绝对尺度、优化几何并估计相机位姿。

Result: 在KITTI、nuScenes和Oxford RobotCar数据集上，在公制度量深度估计、多视角点云重建和跨视图相机定位方面均取得一致改进，且跨数据集和地理区域保持强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 卫星图像作为全局度量参考是一种低成本、可靠且泛化性强的解决尺度模糊的方案，适用于实际公制度量3D重建应用。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models have recently shown strong generalization across diverse scenes, yet most of them recover geometry only up to an unknown global scale. This scale ambiguity limits their use in applications that require metric understanding of the environment. Existing metric reconstruction methods commonly rely on large-scale metric annotations or accurate camera calibration, both of which are costly or unreliable in many real-world settings. We propose a satellite-guided framework for resolving scale ambiguity in feed-forward 3D reconstruction. The key idea is to use readily available satellite imagery as a global metric reference. Given a coarse camera pose, our method retrieves a local satellite patch and integrates it with a feed-forward reconstruction backbone through bidirectional cross-view interaction. By enforcing consistency between the reconstructed scene and the satellite reference, the model infers absolute scale, refines scene geometry, and estimates camera pose in a metric coordinate frame. Experiments on KITTI, nuScenes, and Oxford RobotCar show consistent improvements in metric depth estimation, multi-view point-cloud reconstruction, and cross-view camera localization, while preserving strong generalization across datasets and geographic regions.

</details>


### [71] [SegmentAnyTreeV2: Scaling Transformer-Based Tree Instance Segmentation Across Sensors, Platforms, and Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08206)
*Maciej Wielgosz,Stefano Puliti,Rasmus Astrup*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegmentAnyTreeV2是一个传感器和平台无关的森林点云语义与实例分割框架，采用基于序列化的Point Transformer v3骨干网络、轻量级语义头以及树关注交叉注意力掩码解码器。通过语义预测限制实例解码于树木类体素，结合实例感知查询初始化、一对多种子监督和非对称掩码评分，提升了密集复杂林分中的分离能力。同时引入FOR-instance v3扩展基准（427场景、26,496棵标注树）。在FOR-instanceV2测试集上，精度90.5%、召回80.2%、F1 85.0%、覆盖率90.7%、语义mIoU 87.6%，零样本评估展现强跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有森林点云分割方法在传感器、平台和森林结构多样性方面缺乏通用性，尤其在密集复杂林分中实例分离效果不佳，且缺乏大规模、多样化的标注基准。

Method: 采用序列化Point Transformer v3骨干网络，结合轻量级语义头和树关注交叉注意力掩码解码器；语义预测约束实例解码范围；实例感知查询初始化、一对多种子监督、非对称掩码评分策略；构建FOR-instance v3多模态基准数据集。

Result: 在FOR-instanceV2测试集上：精度90.5%、召回80.2%、F1 85.0%、覆盖率90.7%、语义mIoU 87.6%。零样本跨域评估表现优异。

Conclusion: SegmentAnyTreeV2实现了传感器与平台无关的森林点云分割，在密集林分中实例分离效果显著提升，且具备强跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: We present SegmentAnyTreeV2, a sensor- and platform-agnostic framework for semantic and instance segmentation of forest point clouds. The model combines a serialization-based Point Transformer v3 backbone with a lightweight semantic head and a tree-focused cross-attention mask decoder. Semantic predictions restrict instance decoding to tree-class voxels, while instance-aware query initialization, one-to-many seed supervision, and asymmetric mask scoring improve separation in dense and structurally complex stands. We further introduce FOR-instance v3, an expanded benchmark comprising 427 scenes and 26,496 annotated trees across diverse biomes, forest structures, and LiDAR platforms. On the FOR-instanceV2 test split, SegmentAnyTreeV2 achieves 90.5% precision, 80.2% recall, 85.0% F1, 90.7% coverage, and 87.6% semantic mIoU, outperforming previous learning-based methods in both instance detection and mask completeness. Zero-shot evaluation on independent sites further demonstrates strong cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [72] [Test-Time Scaling in Multimodal Foundation Models: A Comprehensive Survey of Generation and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08231)
*Cong Wan,Ying He,Zhongzhan Huang,Hefeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了多模态基础模型（MFMs）中的测试时扩展（TTS）研究，提出了一个统一分类框架，将现有方法归纳为基于采样、基于反馈和基于搜索三种策略，并总结了代表性应用、基准测试以及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态TTS领域发展迅速，但缺乏系统性综述和统一的理论框架来描绘其发展全貌，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出统一分类框架，将多模态TTS方法分为三类：基于采样（sampling-based）、基于反馈（feedback-based）和基于搜索（search-based）的策略，并总结了代表性应用和基准测试。

Result: 完成了对多模态TTS领域的首次全面综述，建立了统一分类体系，梳理了各类方法的代表性工作和评估基准。

Conclusion: 该综述为多模态TTS研究提供了系统性的路线图，指出了当前开放挑战和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Test-time Scaling (TTS) has emerged as a pivotal research direction for enhancing model performance by dynamically allocating computational resources during inference. Recent advancements have adapted this paradigm to Multimodal Foundation Models (MFMs), unlocking their potential in multimodal reasoning and generation. Despite rapid progress, the field lacks a systematic survey and unified theoretical framework to delineate the developmental landscape of multimodal TTS. To bridge this gap, we present the first comprehensive review of TTS research for MFMs, proposing a unified taxonomic framework that categorizes existing methodologies into three distinct strategies: sampling-based, feedback-based, and search-based approaches. We further summarize representative applications and benchmarks commonly utilized to evaluate multimodal TTS capabilities in generation and reasoning tasks. Finally, this survey discusses open challenges and outlines future research directions, providing a systematic roadmap for subsequent studies in this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [73] [Light-WAM: Efficient World Action Models with State-Fusion Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08242)
*Ziang Li,Dongzhou Cheng,Yibin Wang,Shiyue Wang,Xiaoyang Xu,Lingxuan Weng,Juan Wang,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Light-WAM是一种轻量级世界动作模型，通过紧凑视频骨干网络和降采样潜空间未来视频监督降低训练成本，并引入StateFusionActionExpert实现高效动作预测，在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上取得良好性能，仅0.44B参数、72.03ms推理延迟、4.1GiB峰值显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）依赖大规模生成架构，训练成本高、推理延迟大，难以部署为高效的闭环策略。

Method: 1) 使用紧凑视频骨干网络，在降采样潜空间进行未来视频监督；2) 提出StateFusionActionExpert，从多层骨干读取适应状态，通过学习查询池化融合，单次前向直接预测动作块。

Result: 在LIBERO上保持强性能，在RoboTwin 2.0上实现可用多任务性能，仅0.44B可训练参数，72.03ms推理延迟，4.1GiB峰值GPU显存，训练吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: Light-WAM通过轻量化设计有效降低了WAM的训练和推理成本，同时保持了良好的任务表现，为高效闭环机器人策略部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) extend robot policy learning by incorporating future prediction as an additional training objective, encouraging the policy to encode task-relevant temporal structure in its representations. Current WAMs often rely on large-scale generative architectures that incur high training costs and inference latency, making them difficult to deploy as efficient closed-loop policies. We propose Light-WAM, a lightweight World Action Model for efficient robot manipulation. Specifically, it is built with a compact video backbone and performs future-video supervision in a downsampled latent space, reducing the cost of video co-training while retaining its benefits for representation learning. For action prediction, Light-WAM introduces the StateFusionActionExpert, which reads adapted states from multiple backbone layers, fuses them through learned-query pooling, and directly predicts action chunks in a single forward pass. This design provides an efficient interface between video backbone representations and robot actions, avoiding the need for heavy generative action experts. Experiments demonstrate that Light-WAM maintains strong performance on LIBERO and achieves usable multi-task performance on RoboTwin 2.0, while using only 0.44B trainable parameters. It also achieves 72.03ms inference latency with 4.1GiB peak GPU memory and improved training throughput.

</details>


### [74] [TIDE: Task-Isolated Diffusion for Unified Video Editing and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08260)
*Qi Liu,Gang Yue,Mingyu Yin,Lisai Zhang,Yidi Wu,Yaole Wang,Yaohui Wang,Chang Yao,Jingyuan Chen,Lin Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TIDE提出了一个统一的视频生成与编辑框架，通过逐令牌任务嵌入、双路径条件机制和多任务渐进训练策略，在指令编辑、参考引导编辑和多参考生成等任务上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
