Motivation: 现有视频生成与编辑模型通常是任务特定的，缺乏统一框架；已有的统一尝试要么需要专用辅助编码器，要么缺少区分异质条件令牌的显式机制，难以处理不同任务中视觉条件数量和类型的变化。

Method: 1）逐令牌任务嵌入：为每个输入令牌分配任务特定标识，显式区分目标、源和参考令牌；2）双路径条件方案：结合视觉-语言模型与VAE潜路径，同时捕获高层语义理解和细粒度结构保真度；3）多任务渐进训练策略：逐步引入复杂度递增的任务，协调多样化目标并实现跨异构任务分布的平滑泛化。

Result: 在多个视频编辑和生成基准测试上的大量实验表明，TIDE在所有评估任务上均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: TIDE通过统一框架有效解决了视频生成与编辑的多样任务，验证了逐令牌任务嵌入和双路径条件机制在区分异质条件方面的有效性，为构建通用视频基础模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Diffusion Transformers have driven rapid progress in video generation and editing, yet these capabilities are still handled by separate, task-specific models. Building a unified framework that supports diverse video tasks remains an open challenge: existing unified attempts either require dedicated auxiliary encoders or lack explicit mechanisms to distinguish heterogeneous conditioning tokens, struggling when the number and type of visual conditions vary across tasks. We propose TIDE, a unified framework that integrates instruction-based editing, reference-guided editing, and multi-reference generation. At its core, we introduce per-token task embeddings that assign each input token a task-specific identifier, enabling the model to explicitly disambiguate target, source, and reference tokens. To simultaneously capture high-level semantic understanding and fine-grained structural fidelity, we design a dual-path conditioning scheme that couples a vision-language model with a VAE latent path for complementary signals. We further devise a multi-task progressive training strategy that incrementally introduces tasks of increasing complexity, effectively harmonizing diverse objectives and enabling smooth generalization across heterogeneous task distributions. Extensive experiments on multiple video editing and generation benchmarks demonstrate that TIDE achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated tasks. Our project page is available at https://LittleWork123.github.io/tide.

</details>


### [75] [Remember with Confidence: Uncertainty Quantification for Spatio-temporal Memory with Probabilistic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08277)
*Harry Zhang,Nicolas Gorlo,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于对象级语义不确定性的多视角VLM记忆系统UQ-DAAAM，通过量化跨视角语义不一致性来检测不可靠描述，并在固定查询预算下主动优化高不确定性对象，显著提升了4D记忆系统的可靠性和问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景图和检索增强系统将VLM描述视为可靠来源，但VLM字幕存在噪声和视角不一致问题，且缺乏检测不可靠存储描述的机制，导致长时域机器人操作中的空间语义记忆不可靠。

Method: 提出对象级语义不确定性指标，量化多视角VLM记忆的跨视角语义散度；构建UQ-DAAAM系统，利用该分数在固定查询预算下主动选择高质量视角进行优化，并将多视角字幕融合为单一对象描述；导出概率保证证明高质量候选视角更可能降低不确定性。

Result: 在OC-NaVQA基准上，UQ-DAAAM相比基线方法实现了更大的不确定性降低和更好的时空问答性能。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化能够使具身4D记忆系统更可靠、更有效，为处理多视角VLM描述中的噪声和一致性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot operation requires spatio-temporal memory to record the environment state and recall it for downstream reasoning. Scene graphs and retrieval-augmented systems ground VLM descriptions to persistent 3D entities with rich semantic descriptions. However, VLM captions are noisy and viewpoint-inconsistent, and existing systems treat them as an oracle with no mechanism to detect unreliable stored descriptions. We introduce object-level semantic uncertainty for multi-view VLM memory: a score that measures object-centric cross-view semantic scatter of captions and identifies semantically unresolved objects. Then, we include our uncertainty scores in an advanced spatial-semantic memory system, that we dub UQ-DAAAM. UQ-DAAAM uses this score to actively refine uncertain objects under a fixed query budget by selecting high-quality views and fusing the resulting multi-view captions into a single object description. We also derive probabilistic guarantees showing that higher-quality candidate views (as selected by our approach) are more likely to reduce uncertainty. Our experiments show that uncertainty quantification can make embodied 4D memory systems more reliable and more effective. In particular, on the OC-NaVQA benchmark, UQ-DAAAM achieves substantially larger uncertainty reduction and better spatio-temporal question answering performance than baselines.

</details>


### [76] [G2G: Exploiting Intra-Group Geometry for Inter-Group Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08284)
*Yufei Wei,Shuhao Ye,Chenxiao Hu,Yiyuan Pan,Dongyu Feng,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang,Yanmei Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出G2G方法，在保持预训练多视图基础模型完全冻结的情况下，仅增加约3200万参数（不到完整模型的6%）的三个轻量级可训练模块（感知器重采样器、跨组桥接模块和多帧位姿头），用于恢复两组图像之间的相对6-DoF位姿，在四个数据集上达到SOTA精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多视图视为无序集合处理，缺乏跨组推理能力，无法有效利用已知的组内几何信息来恢复两组图像间的相对位姿。

Method: 保持预训练多视图基础模型完全冻结，新增三个轻量级可训练模块：1) 感知器重采样器（perceiver resampler）；2) 跨组桥接模块，包含合并自注意力（merged self-attention）；3) 多帧位姿头。仅需相对位姿作为监督信号。

Result: 在四个数据集（涵盖室内外模拟、真实世界跨季节采集以及零样本sim-to-real迁移）上，在跨序列重定位和多相机里程计两项任务中均达到SOTA精度，且所有基线均使用完整的原始监督信号重新训练。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级可训练模块桥接两组图像，在不微调基础模型的情况下实现高效的跨组相对位姿估计，验证了该方法的有效性、泛化性和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Recovering the relative 6-DoF pose between two image groups underlies cross-sequence relocalization and multi-camera rig odometry. Each group carries known intra-group geometry from visual odometry or rig calibration, and pretrained multi-view backbones already fuse such geometry into visual features. Yet current models treat all views as an unstructured set, leaving cross-group reasoning as the missing piece. We introduce \ours{}, which keeps the foundation model entirely frozen and adds three lightweight trainable modules to bridge the two groups: a perceiver resampler, a cross-group bridge with merged self-attention, and a multi-frame pose head. The trainable footprint totals about 32M parameters, under 6\% of the full model, and is supervised only by relative poses. Across four datasets that span indoor and outdoor simulation, real-world cross-season capture, and zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, \ours{} attains state-of-the-art accuracy on both tasks, while every baseline is retrained with its full original supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/WeiYuFei0217/G2G.

</details>


### [77] [HACK++: Towards More Effective Head-Aware Key-Value Compression for Efficient Visual Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08302)
*Ziran Qin,Yuchen Jiang,Mingbao Lin,Youru Lv,Hang Guo,Wen Fei,Weiyao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对VAR模型跨尺度KV缓存累积导致的高注意力复杂度和内存开销问题，提出HACK++：一种无需训练的头感知键值缓存压缩框架。通过离线校准将注意力头分为上下文头（保持语义一致性）和结构头（保持空间连贯性），并为两类头设计自适应缓存预算分配策略。在Infinity-2B/8B上，使用30%注意力预算和10%缓存预算即可保持近乎无损的生成质量，甚至在1%缓存预算下仍表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAR模型因跨尺度KV缓存累积面临显著注意力复杂度和严重内存开销，而现有的一刀切式压缩方法在VAR模型上表现不佳，因为VAR注意力头可稳定分为两类功能不同的头（上下文头与结构头），它们对历史尺度的依赖不同且随层和生成步变化。

Method: 提出HACK++框架：1）通过一次性离线校准分类头类型并导出头特定先验；2）推理时将注意力与缓存压缩解耦，在独立预算下运行；3）通过模式特定策略和依赖感知的预算分配，限制当前尺度注意力成本并更激进地压缩累积缓存。

Result: 在多个VAR模型（文本到图像、类别条件、统一理解与生成任务）上验证有效性和泛化性。例如Infinity-2B/8B上，30%注意力预算+10%缓存预算维持近乎无损，1%缓存预算仍鲁棒。

Conclusion: HACK++通过头感知的差异化缓存压缩策略，有效解决了VAR模型跨尺度KV缓存开销问题，在极低预算下仍能保持生成质量。

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (VAR) models adopt a next-scale prediction paradigm, offering high-quality generation with substantially fewer decoding steps. However, existing VAR models suffer from significant attention complexity and severe memory overhead due to the accumulation of key-value (KV) caches across scales. In this paper, we tackle this challenge by introducing KV cache compression into the next-scale paradigm. We begin with an in-depth analysis of VAR attention and observe that attention heads can be stably divided into two functionally distinct categories: Contextual Heads focus on maintaining semantic consistency, while Structural Heads preserve spatial coherence. Their functional divergence makes existing one-size-fits-all compression methods perform poorly on VAR models. We further find that the two head types differ markedly in their reliance on historical scales, and that this reliance shifts across layers and generation steps, arguing for an adaptive cache budget allocation. To address these challenges, we propose HACK++, a training-free Head-Aware key-value Compression frameworK for VAR models. From a one-time offline calibration, HACK++ classifies head types and derives head-specific priors. At inference, it decouples attention from cache compression under independent budgets, bounding the current-scale attention cost while compressing the accumulated cache far more aggressively, via pattern-specific strategies and a reliance-aware budget allocation. Extensive experiments on multiple VAR models across text-to-image, class-conditional, and unified understanding-and-generation tasks validate the effectiveness and generalizability of HACK++. For example, on Infinity-2B/8B, HACK++ maintains near-lossless generation with only a 30% attention budget and a 10% cache budget, and remains robust even under a 1% cache budget.

</details>


### [78] [Set-Based Transformer for Atmospheric Compensation in Standoff LWIR Hyperspectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08324)
*Fabian Perez,Nicolas Quintero,Jeferson Acevedo,Hoover Rueda-Chacon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级基于集合的深度学习框架，利用不同距离的多辐射测量值联合估计透射率、大气路径辐射和共享下行辐射，在MODTRAN生成的LWIR数据集上实现了低光谱失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动远距离LWIR高光谱成像受大气吸收、发射和反射辐射影响，大气补偿至关重要但因其实际和建模难度而被严重忽视。

Method: 提出轻量级基于集合的深度学习框架，以不同距离的多辐射测量值为输入，联合估计透射率、大气路径辐射和共享下行辐射；使用稀疏自编码器分析学习到的表征。

Result: 在MODTRAN生成的远距离LWIR数据集上，所有估计产品均实现低光谱失真；稀疏自编码器观察到多个潜在特征在测试数据的地理相干子集上激活，尽管缺乏位置监督。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了远距离LWIR成像中的大气补偿难题，实现了低光谱失真的联合估计，且学习到的表征具有地理一致性。

Abstract: Passive long-wave infrared (LWIR) hyperspectral imaging under a standoff geometry depends on atmospheric absorption and emission, as well as reflected radiance, thus making atmospheric compensation essential to get knowledge of a target of interest. Despite its importance, this compensation has been largely overlooked due to its practical and modeling difficulty. In this paper, we present a lightweight set-based deep learning framework that takes multiple radiance measurements, collected at different standoff ranges, as input and jointly estimates transmittance, atmospheric path radiance, and a shared downwelling spectrum. We analyze the learned representation with a sparse autoencoder and observe that several latent features do activate on geographically coherent subsets of the test data despite the absence of location supervision. Experiments on a MODTRAN generated standoff LWIR dataset demonstrate low spectral distortion across all estimated products. The dataset and code is publicly available at: https://factral.co/SAE-LWIR/

</details>


### [79] [SMI: Efficient Self-Supervised Learning via Mutual-Information-Inspired Dependency Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08332)
*Pritam Mishra,Coloma Ballester,Dimosthenis Karatzas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义互信息（SMI），一种轻量级自监督学习目标，通过在样本级依赖矩阵上进行非线性优化，在降低计算复杂度的同时取得了与SOTA方法竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL方法依赖大batch size、memory bank、动量编码器或全局同步机制，导致计算成本和训练复杂度高，亟需一种计算高效的替代方案。

Method: 在高斯假设下基于互信息推导出语义互信息（SMI）目标函数，通过非线性变换将样本对相关性映射为依赖矩阵，在样本级而非特征级进行优化，强调强依赖语义对的同时保持表示多样性。

Result: 在ImageNet（ResNet-50）上线性评估达到竞争性能；在多个低资源微调基准上持续优于Barlow Twins，尤其细粒度数据集；优化动力学和表示几何分析表明更好的对齐-冗余平衡、更高的特征多样性和更空间局部的语义表示。

Conclusion: 非线性依赖优化为传统基于相关性的自监督学习目标提供了一种有效且计算高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has achieved remarkable representation learning performance, but many existing methods rely on large batch sizes, memory banks, momentum encoders, or global synchronization mechanisms that substantially increase computational cost and training complexity. In this work, we propose Semantic Mutual Information (SMI), a lightweight self-supervised objective derived from a mutual-information-inspired dependency formulation under Gaussian assumptions. Unlike conventional correlation matching objectives that operate on high-dimensional feature correlation matrices, SMI performs optimization on a sample-level dependency matrix through a nonlinear transformation of pairwise correlations. This formulation induces distinct optimization dynamics that emphasize strongly dependent semantic pairs while maintaining representation diversity. Experimental results on ImageNet using a ResNet-50 backbone demonstrate that SMI achieves competitive linear evaluation performance relative to state-of-the-art SSL approaches while substantially reducing computational complexity. Across multiple low-resource benchmarks, SMI consistently improves transfer performance over Barlow Twins, particularly on fine-grained datasets. Furthermore, analyses of optimization dynamics and representation geometry suggest improved alignment--redundancy balance, greater feature diversity, and more spatially localized semantic representations. These results indicate that nonlinear dependency optimization provides an effective and computationally efficient alternative to conventional correlation-based self-supervised learning objectives.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Raw Signals: Undecoded Generative Latents as Privileged Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08336)
*Cristian Sbrolli,Nicolas Michel,Matteo Matteucci,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态集成计算成本高且依赖完美配对数据的问题，本文提出直接潜在增强（DLA）方法，绕过“解码-编码循环”，直接利用生成式AI的潜在表示作为特权信息；同时提出多层显式模拟联觉（MESSy）方法，通过预测目标将潜在知识迁移至纯视觉学生模型，避免刚性表示匹配带来的特征失真。实验表明该方法显著优于原始数据增强和传统蒸馏，使单模态学生模型具备与物理属性对齐的“联觉”潜在结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态集成虽能显著提升视觉模型性能，但部署成本高昂且需要稀缺的完美配对数据集。现有方法通过生成式AI合成缺失模态来解决数据瓶颈，却引入了“解码-编码循环”这一严重效率问题：信息丰富的生成潜在表示被解码为带噪声的原始信号，迫使下游分类器浪费容量重新编码。

Method: 提出DLA（直接潜在增强），直接利用未解码的生成潜在表示作为特权信息；以及MESSy（多层显式模拟联觉），通过预测目标而非刚性表示匹配，将密集知识安全地内化到纯视觉学生模型中。

Result: 所提框架显著优于原始数据增强和传统蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 本文方法能训练出高精度的单模态学生模型，其潜在结构具有“联觉”特性，即与从未直接观察到的物理属性保持内在对齐。

Abstract: While multimodal integration significantly improves computer vision models, deploying them incurs prohibitive inference costs and requires scarce, perfectly paired datasets. Recent methods address this data bottleneck by synthesizing missing modalities via generative AI, yet they introduce a severe inefficiency: the Decode-Encode Loop. Specifically, information-rich generative latents are decoded into noisy raw signals, forcing the downstream classifier to waste capacity re-encoding them. To bypass this bottleneck, we propose Direct Latent Augmentation (DLA), utilizing undecoded generative latents directly as privileged information. Furthermore, to transfer this dense knowledge to a purely visual student, we introduce Multilayer Explicit Simulated Synesthesia (MESSy). Instead of enforcing rigid representation matching, which forces the student to distort its native visual features to accommodate complex multimodal topologies, MESSy uses a predictive objective to safely internalize these physical priors. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms raw data augmentation and traditional distillation. Ultimately, our approach yields highly accurate unimodal students with ``synesthetic'' latent structures that are inherently aligned with physical properties they have never directly observed.

</details>


### [81] [Self-Supervised Vision Transformers for CBCT-Based Detection of Temporomandibular Joint Osteoarthritis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08364)
*Shradhdha Trivedi,Vrundan Sojitra,Mariela Padilla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了DINO系列自监督视觉基础模型（DINOv1、DINOv2、DINOv2+reg、RAD-DINO）在颞下颌关节骨关节炎（TMJ OA）锥形束CT（CBCT）图像分类中的迁移学习效果，提出基于切片的ViT+注意力MIL分类框架。通过系统性消融实验发现：部分解冻ViT最后两个Transformer块是决定性因素，可将AUC从0.671（完全冻结DINOv2）提升至0.902，优于DINOv1（0.867）、DINOv2+reg（0.774）和监督ImageNet基线（0.843），表明适应策略比骨干网络选择更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMJ OA的CBCT影像中骨质变化细微，自动检测具有挑战性。现有的DINO系列自监督ViT模型在医学影像上的迁移效果缺乏系统研究，特别是CBCT这种低数据量、三维影像场景中骨干网络的适应策略（冻结/解冻程度）尚未明确。

Method: 提出基于切片的ViT+注意力MIL分类框架：将轴向CBCT切片逐片输入冻结或部分解冻的ViT编码，然后通过注意力多实例学习（MIL）聚合切片级特征进行患者级二分类（OA/正常）。在多个CBCT来源数据集上系统消融不同解冻策略和聚合设计。

Result: 部分解冻ViT最后两个Transformer块是关键因素，AUC从0.671（完全冻结DINOv2）提升至0.902，超越DINOv1（0.867）、DINOv2+reg（0.774）、RAD-DINO及监督ImageNet ViT-B/16基线（0.843）。

Conclusion: 在低数据医学影像场景中，DINO系列基础模型的适应策略（特别是部分解冻最后两个Transformer块）比骨干网络选择本身更能驱动性能提升，为CBCT等三维医学影像的迁移学习提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Temporomandibular joint osteoarthritis (TMJ OA) is a prevalent degenerative condition whose osseous changes are often subtle on cone-beam CT (CBCT), making automated detection challenging. We study how well the DINO family of self-supervised vision transformers -- DINOv1, DINOv2, DINOv2+reg, and RAD-DINO (a radiology-pretrained variant) -- transfers to CBCT, asking how much backbone adaptation is needed and of what kind. We propose a simple slice-based pipeline using Vision Transformer (ViT) backbones: axial CBCT slices are encoded per-slice by a frozen or partially adapted ViT and aggregated via attention-based multiple instance learning (MIL) for patient-level binary OA/Normal classification. Through systematic ablation across unfreezing strategies and aggregation designs on a multi-source CBCT dataset, we find that partial unfreezing of the final two transformer blocks is the decisive factor, improving AUC from 0.671 (fully frozen DINOv2) to 0.902. This outperforms DINOv1 (0.867), DINOv2+reg (0.774), and a supervised ImageNet ViT-B/16 baseline (0.843). Our results provide practical guidance for adapting DINO-family foundation models in low-data medical imaging settings, showing that adaptation strategy is a stronger driver of performance than backbone choice alone.

</details>


### [82] [SceneConductor: 3D Scene Generation from Single Image with Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08402)
*Jeonghwan Kim,Yushi Lan,Yongwei Chen,Hieu Trung Nguyen,Chuanyu Pan,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体编排框架，将单图像3D场景生成分解为场景初始化、环境构建和多智能体细化三阶段，通过几何感知布局预测器和稀疏几何先验实现高质量3D重建，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖整体或弱分解的流水线，耦合多种因素且需要大量场景级监督，限制了其在复杂真实环境中的泛化能力。

Method: 提出三阶段多智能体框架：(1) 初始化阶段：提取图像掩码、构建物体级3D表示并预测初始空间布局；(2) 环境构建阶段：利用初始化结果和点图几何构建支撑面、房间边界、材质和光照；(3) 细化阶段：规划智能体识别不一致性，直接修正或分派专家智能体进行局部修订并重新整合。还引入几何感知布局预测器，利用点图稀疏几何先验进行监督。

Result: 在基准数据集上，所提方法在几何精度、空间一致性和感知真实感方面一致优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 通过结构化解耦和多智能体协作，有效减少了对场景级标注的依赖，提升了对多样真实场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Generating complete 3D scenes from a single image requires inferring globally consistent geometry, object relationships, and environmental context from inherently ambiguous visual evidence. Despite recent progress in joint layout-and-mesh generation, existing methods often rely on holistic or weakly decomposed pipelines that entangle many factors at once and demand extensive scene-level supervision, limiting their generalization to complex real-world environments. We propose a multi-agent orchestration framework that decomposes single-image 3D scene generation into three structured stages: scene initialization, environment construction, and multi-agent refinement. The initialization stage extracts image-derived object masks, builds object-level 3D representations, and predicts an initial spatial layout to form a coarse 3D scene. The environment-construction stage then leverages this initialization together with point-map geometry to build an environmental scaffold of supporting surfaces, room boundaries, materials, and illumination. Finally, in the refinement stage, a planner agent identifies structural and visual inconsistencies, applies simple corrections directly, and dispatches specialist agents for complex localized revisions that are reintegrated into the global scene. To provide reliable structural initialization while reducing reliance on scene-level annotations, we further introduce a geometry-aware layout predictor supervised by sparse geometric priors derived from point maps. Unlike fully supervised layout generators, the predictor can be trained from segmentation-level data and generalizes robustly to diverse real-world scenes. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our method consistently outperforms prior approaches in geometric accuracy, spatial consistency, and perceptual realism.

</details>


### [83] [Geometry-Driven Flow Analysis of Brain Sulcal Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08404)
*Moo K. Chung,Luigi Maccotta,Aaron Struck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于泊松方程的皮层折叠分析框架，通过将折叠模式建模为几何驱动的源-汇结构，生成光滑平衡的势场，实现对青少年肌阵挛癫痫（JME）患者皮层折叠几何结构的物理可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有皮层折叠分析依赖间接标量摘要，未能显式建模折叠几何本身；JME患者的皮层异常通常微妙、空间分布广泛，常规形态测量难以检测。

Method: 提出基于泊松方程的框架：将皮层折叠建模为源自平均曲率的几何驱动流，将折叠模式视为稳态源-汇结构，生成光滑全局平衡的势场，其表面梯度定义物理可解释的通量。

Result: 该方法实现了对脑沟-脑回折叠组织的空间连贯分析，提供了JME中几何驱动皮层结构的原理性表示。

Conclusion: 该框架能够更敏感地检测JME等神经疾病中微妙且空间分布的皮层折叠异常，为神经疾病提供新的生物标志物。

Abstract: Cortical folding reflects coordinated neurodevelopmental processes and is increasingly recognized as a sensitive marker of neurological disease. However, most existing analyses rely on indirect scalar summaries that do not explicitly model folding geometry itself. In juvenile myoclonic epilepsy (JME), a common genetic epilepsy, cortical abnormalities are often subtle, spatially distributed, and difficult to detect using conventional morphometric measures. We introduce a Poisson-equation-based framework that models cortical folding as a geometry-driven flow derived from mean curvature on the cortical manifold. By treating folding patterns as a stationary source-sink structure, the proposed approach yields a smooth, globally balanced potential field whose surface gradient defines a physically interpretable flux. This framework enables spatially coherent analysis of sulcal-gyral folding organization and provides a principled representation of geometry-driven cortical structure in JME.

</details>


### [84] [CoVEBench: Can Video Editing Models Handle Complex Instructions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08415)
*Jiangtao Wu,Jiaming Wang,Yiwen He,Yuanxing Zhang,Shihao Li,Dunyuan Liu,Xuedong Zhao,Jialu Chen,Zekun Moore Wang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CoVEBench，一个组合式视频编辑基准测试，包含 416 个源视频、626 个多点编辑指令和 9,990 个细粒度检查项，用于评估模型在复杂组合编辑任务中的表现。实验表明现有模型在处理多个并发编辑操作时存在严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导视频编辑模型在单一任务（如风格迁移、物体插入）上表现优异，但真实用户请求往往是高度组合性的（如同时修改主体、动作和相机视角）。现有基准测试受限于孤立编辑场景和粗糙的全局指标，无法诊断模型在复杂组合工作流中的表现。

Method: 构建了 CoVEBench 基准测试，包含 416 个源视频、626 个多点编辑指令和 9,990 个细粒度检查项。评估方式结合多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对指令遵循度和视频保真度的评判，以及自动化视频质量指标。

Result: 实验揭示组合式视频编辑仍是重大挑战：现有模型在处理多个并发操作时，经常出现遗漏编辑、违反保真约束或引入伪影等问题。

Conclusion: CoVEBench 为视频编辑领域提供了一个具有挑战性的诊断性测试平台，有助于推动模型向更贴近真实用户需求的组合式编辑能力发展。

Abstract: While recent text-guided video editing models excel at elementary tasks (e.g., style transfer, object insertion), real-world user requests are highly compositional. A single prompt often demands multiple coupled edits, such as modifying subjects, actions, and camera views, while strictly preserving unrelated spatiotemporal content. Existing benchmarks, heavily constrained by isolated edits and coarse global metrics, fail to diagnose how models handle such complex workflows. To address this gap, we introduce CoVEBench, a compositional video editing benchmark comprising 416 curated source videos, 626 multi-point editing instructions, and 9,990 fine-grained checklist items. Covering diverse editing dimensions, CoVEBench evaluates models via MLLM-judged instruction compliance and video fidelity, alongside automated metrics for video quality. Extensive experiments reveal that compositional editing remains a profound challenge: current models frequently omit edits, violate preservation constraints, or introduce artifacts when handling multiple operations simultaneously. CoVEBench provides a challenging, diagnostic testbed to advance video editing toward realistic user workflows.

</details>


### [85] [CheXanatomy: Anatomy-Aware Vision-Language Modeling for Chest Radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08420)
*Sergios Gatidis,Curtis Langlotz,Christian Bluethgen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CheXanatomy框架通过在预训练视觉-语言模型的自回归token空间中注入解剖知识，使其能够通过下一个token预测生成解剖分割掩码，无需专用解码头。在合成和真实胸部X光片上，该方法性能与U-Net相当，在域偏移下表现出更好的几何鲁棒性，并提高了样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型擅长图像级全局对齐，但缺乏细粒度解剖结构编码，难以用于分割等空间精确任务。

Method: 1) 在预训练VLM中通过自回归token空间监督注入解剖知识；2) 通过CT体积合成逼真的胸部X光片并前向投影CT分割标签，获得解剖一致的2D掩码用于可扩展监督；3) 模型通过下一个token预测训练生成解剖分割掩码。

Result: 1) 与U-Net在分布内性能相当；2) 在域偏移到真实CXR数据时表现出更好的几何鲁棒性；3) 解剖预训练模型在新定位任务中样本效率更高；4) 更大模型和更高分辨率提升性能，视觉编码器微调效果有限。

Conclusion: 将解剖结构直接嵌入生成目标能促进空间有表征的表示，支持解剖感知的医学视觉-语言建模。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) pretrained on large-scale image-text pairs demonstrate strong image-level understanding, but are primarily optimized for global alignment and do not explicitly encode fine-grained anatomical structure, limiting their suitability for spatially precise tasks such as segmentation. We introduce CheXanatomy, a framework that integrates explicit anatomical knowledge into a pretrained VLM through autoregressive token-space supervision. Instead of adding task-specific decoder heads, the model is trained to generate anatomical segmentation masks via next-token prediction. To enable scalable supervision, we synthesize realistic chest radiographs from CT volumes and forward-project CT segmentation labels to obtain anatomically consistent 2D masks. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and real chest radiographs against a U-Net baseline, including ablations on model scale, input resolution, and vision encoder fine-tuning. Autoregressive anatomical supervision achieves performance comparable to specialized convolutional models in-distribution and demonstrates improved geometric robustness under domain shift to real CXR data. In addition, anatomy-pretrained models exhibit improved sample efficiency when adapting to novel localization tasks under limited supervision. Larger models and higher input image resolution improve performance, while vision encoder fine-tuning has limited effect. These results show that embedding anatomical structure directly into the generative objective promotes spatially grounded representations and supports anatomy-aware medical vision-language modeling.

</details>


### [86] [Segmentation-Assisted Brain MRI Synthesis with Cross-Image Multi-Contrast Feature Memory Bank Retrieval Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08421)
*Wenwei Huang,Jia Wei,Jianlong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于检索增强合成的闭环合成-分割框架（SSCF），通过生成对抗网络架构、辅助分割分支和双库检索增强策略，有效解决多对比度脑MRI中缺失模态的图像合成问题，尤其提升了肿瘤区域的合成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多对比度脑MRI合成中难以有效合成关键肿瘤区域，且无法充分利用多对比度图像中的上下文信息。

Method: 采用生成对抗网络架构，以单一模型从任意可用对比度合成缺失对比度；引入辅助分割分支预测肿瘤掩膜并反馈至合成分支作为语义条件，实现肿瘤感知表示学习；提出双库检索增强策略，动态查询肿瘤掩膜记忆库（获取肿瘤上下文）和跨对比度特征记忆库（获取全局风格信息）以增强合成效果。

Result: 在BraTS2020和UCSF-BMSR两个公开数据集上验证，方法在处理脑部医学图像合成任务中有效，性能优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的合成-分割闭环框架结合检索增强，显著提升了多对比度脑MRI中肿瘤区域的合成质量，证明了方法的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Multi-contrast brain MRI provide complementary soft-tissue characteristics that aid in the screening and diagnosis of diseases. However, limited scanning time, image corruption and various imaging protocols often result in incomplete multi-contrast images. While current approaches excel in image synthesis, they often struggle to synthesize critical tumor regions and exploit contextual information in multi-contrast brain MRI effectively. To address this issue, we propose a synthesis-centric, segmentation-assisted closed-loop framework with retrieval augmentation synthesis. Our method overall takes a generative adversarial architecture, which aims to synthesize missing contrasts from any combination of available ones with a single model. To explicitly capture tumor semantics and focus synthesis on tumor regions, we add an auxiliary segmentation branch that predicts tumor masks and feeds them back as semantic conditioning in synthesis branch, thereby learning tumor-aware representations in the model and improving synthesis fidelity. Furthermore, we propose a dual-bank retrieval augmentation strategy. It dynamically queries two external knowledge bases, namely a tumor masks memory bank for crucial tumor context and cross-image contrast feature memory bank for global style information, to augment synthesis. Verified on two public multi-contrast magnetic resonance brain datasets: BraTs2020 and UCSF-BMSR, the proposed method is effective in handling medical brain images synthesis tasks and shows superior performance compared to previous methods. Code is available at:https://github.com/iBizzard/SSCF.git

</details>


### [87] [Reinforcing Temporal Answer Grounding in Instructional Video via Candidate-Aware Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08436)
*Muge Qi,Rong Fu,Pengbin Feng,Xianda Li,Yu Cai,Yifu Guo,Shizhe Zhang,Simon James Fong,Lei Ma,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种候选感知因果推理（CACR）框架，通过视觉语言预训练的候选选择算法和时序逻辑推理模块，有效解决了教学视频中时间答案定位任务中语义理解复杂和视频长度不匹配的问题，在六个基准数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学视频中的时间答案定位（TAGV）任务面临语义复杂问题理解和未剪辑视频与短目标片段之间长度严重不匹配的挑战，现有方法对无关内容敏感且视觉推理能力不足。

Method: 提出CACR框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 基于视觉语言预训练的候选选择（VBCS）算法，高效生成K个候选片段；(2) 时序逻辑推理模块，引入拒绝奖励机制并使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)进行优化，实现鲁棒推理。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在平均交并比（mIoU）指标上达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: CACR框架为长视频中基于推理的检索提供了新的视角，有效解决了TAGV任务中的关键挑战。

Abstract: The task of temporal answer grounding in instructional video (TAGV), which aims to locate precise video segments that respond to natural language queries, is increasingly important for direct video answer retrieval. This task remains challenging due to the need to comprehend semantically complex questions and to address the significant length mismatch between untrimmed videos and short target moments. Existing methods often suffer from sensitivity to irrelevant content or insufficient visual reasoning capabilities. To tackle these limitations, we propose a Candidate-Aware Causal Reasoning (CACR) framework. Our approach first employs a Visual-Language Pre-training based Candidate Selection (VBCS) algorithm to efficiently generate K candidate segments, then applies a temporal logic reasoning module enhanced by a rejection reward mechanism and optimized via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for robust inference. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in terms of mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU), providing a new perspective for reasoning-based retrieval in long videos.

</details>


### [88] [TVI-CoT: Text-Visual Interleaved Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Multimodal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08464)
*Lianyu Hu,Xiaoyu Ma,Zeqin Liao,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TVI-CoT框架，通过可学习的控制令牌<THINK>、<LOOK>和<ANSWER>，在多模态大语言模型的推理过程中显式地交织文本推理与视觉特征访问，解决了传统CoT方法在推理时无法访问视觉信息的"视觉盲推理"问题。在8个基准测试上取得了SOTA结果，相比基线提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT方法在多模态大语言模型中存在根本性局限：推理完全在文本空间进行，视觉编码后图像信息不再可访问，模型只能基于初始描述进行推理，形成"视觉盲推理"范式，限制了细粒度视觉提取、错误验证和自适应注意力。

Method: TVI-CoT框架引入了三种可学习的控制令牌：<THINK>（文本推理）、<LOOK>（视觉特征访问）和<ANSWER>（答案生成）。这些令牌允许模型在推理过程中动态切换文本推理与视觉定位，根据当前推理状态关注相关图像区域，实现文本推理与视觉特征访问的显式交织。

Result: 在MMMU上提升+6.1%，MathVerse上+3.8%，MathVista上+3.4%，ScienceQA上+3.4%，在8个基准测试中达到MLLM-based CoT方法的最佳结果。

Conclusion: TVI-CoT通过引入可学习控制令牌实现了推理过程中的文本-视觉动态交织，有效解决了多模态CoT中的视觉盲推理问题，显著提升了MLLM在多模态推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has proven effective for enhancing problem-solving in large language models. However, when applied to multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), existing CoT approaches suffer from a fundamental limitation: they perform reasoning entirely in text without accessing visual features during the reasoning process. After initial visual encoding, image information becomes inaccessible, forcing models to reason based solely on whatever was captured in the initial description, which forms a `vision-blind reasoning' paradigm that limits fine-grained visual extraction, error verification, and adaptive attention. We propose Text-Visual Interleaved Chain-of-Thought (TVI-CoT), a framework that enables explicit interleaving of textual reasoning and visual feature access through learnable control tokens <THINK>, <LOOK> and <ANSWER>. These tokens allow dynamic switching between reasoning and visual grounding, attending to relevant image regions conditioned on the evolving reasoning state. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art results among MLLM-based CoT methods and notable performance boost compared to the baseline: +6.1% on MMMU, +3.8% on MathVerse, +3.4% on MathVista, and +3.4% on ScienceQA. Code is available at https://github.com/hulianyuyy/TVI-CoT.

</details>


### [89] [Seeing is Believing: Aligning Prompt Rewriting with Visual Anchors for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08492)
*Xuanyi Liu,Deyi Ji,Junyu Lu,Jing Wang,Qianxiong Xu,Xuhang Chen,Tianrun Chen,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaithRewriter 提出了一种基于视觉锚点的提示增强框架，通过多模态大模型生成中间图像作为视觉线索，结合大语言模型生成视觉接地增强，再蒸馏到小模型实现高效部署，以缩小文本到图像生成中的意图-生成差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 模型存在意图-生成差距（intent-generation gap），用户提示往往简短模糊，现有提示重写方法仅优化流畅性和可读性，缺乏视觉接地（visual grounding），导致重写器过度推断缺失细节，进一步扩大意图-生成差距。

Method: 首先利用多模态大模型（MLLM）从原始提示生成中间图像作为视觉线索；然后将该视觉线索与原始提示结合，输入大规模语言模型（LLM）生成视觉接地的增强描述；最后将这些增强知识蒸馏到小规模语言模型，实现高效部署，使其能够生成有效的 T2I 提示。

Result: 实验表明，FaithRewriter 生成的提示比强基线方法更忠实于用户意图，视觉上更合理，有效缩小了意图-生成差距。

Conclusion: FaithRewriter 通过引入中间视觉锚点实现视觉接地增强，结合知识蒸馏实现高效推理，为缩小 T2I 生成中的意图-生成差距提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the impressive capabilities of text-to-image (T2I) models, an intent-generation gap often persists due to the brevity and ambiguity of user prompts. Existing approaches primarily polish the prompt for fluency and readability. However, the enhancement process still lacks visual grounding. As a result, the rewriter may over-infer missing details, causing an intent-generation gap. To address this limitation, we propose FaithRewriter, a novel prompt-enhancement framework for T2I generation. Specifically, FaithRewriter first leverages a multimodal MLLM to generate an image from the original prompt as an intermediate visual cue. This cue is then combined with the prompt and fed into a large-scale LLM to produce visually grounded augmentations that better reflect how the intended content should appear in images. Finally, these augmentations are distilled into a small-scale LLM for efficient deployment, enhancing its ability to generate effective T2I prompts. Experiments show that FaithRewriter yields prompts that are more faithful to the user intent and more visually plausible than strong baselines, helping narrow the intent-generation gap.

</details>


### [90] [Look Less, Reason More: Block-wise Attention Skipping for Efficient Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08511)
*Jie Ma,Zhike Qiu,Jiayi Ji,Xiaoshuai Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中视觉自注意力在深层冗余的问题，提出训练无关的V-Skip推理范式，通过选择性跳过饱和视觉注意力模块实现块级结构化稀疏，并结合轻量级少样本校准动态调整稀疏路径，在保持94.16%-100.31%性能的同时显著降低推理计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs因长视觉token序列的自注意力二次计算复杂度面临严重推理瓶颈。作者发现深层视觉-视觉自注意力存在"视觉注意力饱和"现象——视觉token在早期层已建立充分的空间结构，后续层的自注意力计算冗余。

Method: 提出V-Skip：1) 将空间交互与语义演化解耦，选择性跳过深层冗余的视觉自注意力模块（保留FFN）；2) 利用轻量级少样本校准动态选择任务最优稀疏路径，无需重新训练。

Result: 在多种MLLM上实现94.16%-100.31%性能保持率，有效实现块级稀疏性，显著降低推理计算成本。

Conclusion: 模型无需丢弃视觉信息，只需在合适的深度"少看"即可实现更高效的推理，挑战了"必须压缩视觉token"的传统思路。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face a significant inference bottleneck due to the quadratic computational cost of self-attention over long visual token sequences. However, we identify a critical inefficiency in current architectures: Visual Attention Saturation. Our analysis reveals that visual tokens rapidly establish their spatial structure and intra-modal relationships in early layers, rendering visual-to-visual self-attention in deeper layers computationally redundant. Conversely, Feed-Forward Networks (FFNs) in these layers remain essential for projecting visual features into the evolving textual semantic space. Leveraging this insight, we present Visual-Skip (V-Skip), a training-free inference paradigm that decouples spatial interaction from semantic evolution. Rather than discarding tokens, V-Skip imposes block-wise structured sparsity by selectively bypassing saturated visual self-attention modules. Furthermore, recognizing that varying downstream tasks demand distinct reasoning depths, V-Skip employs a lightweight, few-shot calibration to dynamically route the task-optimal sparsity path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that V-Skip effectively bypasses redundant vision attention to achieve block-wise sparsity, maintaining a 94.16% to 100.31% performance retention across diverse MLLMs. Ultimately, we prove that to reason more effectively, models do not need to discard what they see -- they simply need to "look less" at the right depth.

</details>


### [91] [OmniTryOn: Video Try-On Anything at Once!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08514)
*Changliang Xia,Chengyou Jia,Minnan Luo,Zhuohang Dang,Xin Shen,Bowen Ping*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Try-On Anything任务，旨在单次推理中同时将多种可穿戴物品迁移到视频人物上；构建了TryAny-Bench基准数据集与评估协议；提出无外部先验的OmniTryOn框架，采用首帧可穿戴缓存策略、STC-RoPE保持时空一致性及渐进式训练策略，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿方法存在两个根本局限：一是仅支持单件衣物迁移，无法同时处理多物品；二是过度依赖显式外部先验（如衣物掩码），破坏了物理动态并降低视觉质量。

Method: 1) 提出Try-On Anything任务及TryAny-Bench基准；2) OmniTryOn框架包含：首帧可穿戴缓存策略（First Frame Wearable Cache）提供多样物品；时空一致性旋转位置编码（STC-RoPE）建立鲁棒时空锚点；渐进式试穿训练策略（GTO）逐步掌握多物品合成。

Result: 在TryAny-Bench上，OmniTryOn显著优于现有专用视频试穿模型和通用视频编辑基线，建立了新的标准。

Conclusion: 提出的OmniTryOn框架有效解决了多物品视频虚拟试穿问题，无需外部先验即可保持时空一致性，为Try-On Anything任务提供了强大解决方案。

Abstract: Although video virtual try-on (VVT) has achieved significant progress, existing methods still exhibit two fundamental limitations: first, they are restricted to single-garment transfer, rendering simultaneous multi-object try-on highly impractical; second, their heavy reliance on explicit external priors (e.g., garment masks) inevitably destroys crucial physical dynamics and degrades visual quality. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes the novel Try-On Anything task, which aims to simultaneously transfer diverse wearable objects onto a person in a video in a single inference pass. To support and standardize this paradigm, we introduce TryAny-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark encompassing a paired video dataset alongside a tailored evaluation protocol. Furthermore, we present OmniTryOn, an external-prior-free generative framework designed to tackle this task. Specifically, OmniTryOn employs a First Frame Wearable Cache strategy, which directly provides diverse wearable objects for the generation process through the initial video frame. To maintain consistency, we propose the Spatiotemporally Consistent RoPE (STC-RoPE), which inherently establishes robust spatiotemporal anchors to strictly preserve complex human motions and background dynamics. Optimized by the proposed Gradual Try-On (GTO) training strategy, our model progressively masters robust multi-object synthesis. Extensive experiments on TryAny-Bench demonstrate that OmniTryOn significantly outperforms existing specialized video virtual try-on models and general video editing baselines, establishing a powerful new standard for the Try-On Anything task. Our dataset, code, and models are available at https://github.com/xcltql666/OminTryOn.

</details>


### [92] [DriveReward: A Comprehensive Dataset and Generative Vision-Language Reward Model for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08525)
*Qimao Chen,Fang Li,Yuechen Luo,Zehan Zhang,Haiyang Sun,Fangzhen Li,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Yang Ji,Jiong Deng,Hongwei Xie,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveReward——一个基于时间锚定视觉引导标注的反事实推理轨迹评估数据集，并训练了专门的视觉语言奖励模型（1B参数），在自动驾驶的RL微调和多模态轨迹选择中取得了与基于规则的奖励计算方法可比的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶奖励模型依赖手工规则或感知真值，泛化能力差且难以支持数据规模化；虽然VLM在其他领域被证明可作为奖励模型，但在驾驶任务中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: (1) 构建DriveReward数据集：通过时间锚定的视觉引导进行严格标注，并采用反事实数据标注方案生成包含多样驾驶风格和错误行为的样本；(2) 训练专门的视觉语言奖励模型（1B参数），用于轨迹评估和奖励对齐。

Result: 现有开源和专有VLM均无法在所有驾驶任务上表现优异；本文训练的1B奖励模型在任务特定奖励对齐上超越了更大的VLM；将该奖励模型集成到RL微调和多模态轨迹评分中，在开环和闭环评估中均达到与基于规则的奖励计算相当的性能。

Conclusion: 专门的视觉语言奖励模型可以替代传统基于规则的奖励函数，在自动驾驶中实现有效的奖励对齐，且小模型（1B）可在特定任务上超越更大规模的通用VLM。

Abstract: Reward models play a pivotal role in reinforcement learning (RL) and multi-modal trajectory selection for autonomous driving. However, acquiring such rewards typically relies on hand-crafted rule-based objectives or perception ground truth, which hinders generalization for data-scaling. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated feasibility as reward models in other domains, their effectiveness in driving tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by (1) introducing DriveReward, a reasoning trajectory evaluation dataset rigorously labeled via temporally-grounded visual guidance, and augmented with counterfactual driving behaviors., (2) alongside a specialized Vision-Language Reward Model. To address the scarcity of failure cases in conventional datasets, we propose a counterfactual data annotation scheme to construct cases encompassing diverse driving styles and erroneous behaviors. Evaluations on our proposed benchmark reveal that even leading open-source and proprietary VLMs fail to excel across all tasks, highlighting significant room for improvement in existing models. Building on these findings, we subsequently tailor a specialized 1B reward model that outperforms larger VLMs on task-specific reward alignment. Finally, we validate our reward model's effectiveness by integrating it into RL finetuning and multi-modal trajectory scoring across multiple baselines, achieving performance comparable to rule-based reward calculations in both open-loop and closed-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [93] [NGram-MoSE: Efficient Remote Sensing Super-Resolution via N-Gram Context and Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08535)
*Yun-Hsuan Huang,Trong-An Bui,Chih-Hung Chuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NGram-MoSE，一种轻量级Transformer架构，通过N-Gram上下文注入和混合专家前馈设计，在遥感图像超分辨率任务中以14倍FLOPs缩减实现31.68 dB PSNR，并在滑坡分割任务中提升mAP@50达4.47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感应用面临空间-时间权衡：高空间分辨率图像获取频率低，而高时间分辨率图像通常较粗糙。现有Transformer超分模型计算开销大，且对有限或有偏的训练数据敏感，导致在分布外条件下鲁棒性下降。

Method: 提出NGram-MoSE轻量级Transformer架构，包含两个核心设计：(1) N-Gram上下文注入，用于增强跨窗口局部一致性并缓解窗口边界伪影；(2) 混合专家前馈设计，通过稀疏激活扩展模型容量而不成比例增加推理成本。

Result: 在地理分布外测试集上PSNR达31.68 dB，FLOPs相比重型Transformer参考降低14倍；在滑坡分割任务中，相比于双三次上采样，mAP@50绝对提升4.47%，且表现出更强的跨尺度一致性。

Conclusion: NGram-MoSE可作为资源受限遥感管线中有效的超分辨率模块，提供鲁棒的泛化能力。

Abstract: Remote sensing applications for environmental monitoring and disaster management are frequently constrained by a spatial--temporal trade-off: imagery with fine spatial detail is often acquired less frequently, whereas more temporally available observations are typically coarser. Single-image super-resolution provides a practical means to enhance coarse imagery without changing acquisition schedules, yet many Transformer-based SR models remain computationally expensive and can be sensitive to limited or geographically biased training data, which degrades robustness under out-of-distribution conditions. This paper presents NGram-MoSE, a lightweight Transformer architecture designed to improve both efficiency and texture continuity. NGram-MoSE introduces N-Gram Context Injection to strengthen cross-window local consistency and mitigate window-boundary artifacts, and incorporates a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) feed-forward design to scale capacity through sparse activation without proportional growth in inference cost. Experiments on a geographically disjoint OOD test set show that NGram-MoSE achieves 31.68\,dB PSNR while reducing FLOPs by \(14\times\) relative to a heavyweight Transformer reference. Downstream evaluation on a landslide segmentation benchmark further demonstrates that restoring degraded inputs to the detector training scale improves performance, yielding a 4.47\% absolute gain in mAP@50 over bicubic upsampling, and exhibits stronger cross-scale consistency under scale extrapolation. These results indicate that NGram-MoSE provides an effective SR module for resource-constrained remote sensing pipelines requiring robust generalization.

</details>


### [94] [Towards Accurate Emotion-Attributed Video Captioning via Fine-grained Emotion-Cause Pair Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08566)
*Weidong Chen,Cheng Ye,Zhendong Mao,Liping Wang,Xinyan Liu,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种细粒度情感-原因对提取框架用于情感视频描述生成，通过概念感知的视觉语义分解和视觉引导的情感可解释学习，实现情感-原因对的交叉耦合提取，在三个数据集上取得最佳性能，如EVC-MSVD数据集上BLEU-2提升4.4%、ROUGE-L提升5.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感视频描述方法使用整体视觉特征挖掘全局情感线索，忽略了视觉情感由特定动机原因诱发这一关键特性（通常仅隐含在核心视频片段中），整体挖掘导致显著的信息冗余和不准确的情感线索。

Method: 1) 概念感知视觉语义分解模块，通过探索场景、对象和运动概念增强视觉特征；2) 视觉引导情感可解释学习模块，利用视觉时间动态引导情感细化，并通过VAD向量约束增强可解释性；3) 通过交叉耦合细化前后的视觉与情感特征实现情感-原因对提取，利用对比损失实现语义强制对齐。

Result: 在三个挑战性数据集上验证了方法的优越性，在EVC-MSVD数据集上BLEU-2提升4.4%，ROUGE-L提升5.4%。

Conclusion: 细粒度情感-原因对提取框架能有效优化复杂语义理解和情感感知，显著提升情感视频描述生成性能。

Abstract: Emotional Video Captioning (EVC) is a challenging task that aims to generate factually accurate and emotionally rich descriptions for videos. Existing EVC methods leverage holistic visual features to mine global emotional cues, and then aggregate multimodal features to guide the emotional caption generation, which ignores the critical characteristic of the EVC task. Visual emotions are evoked by specific motivational causes, which are usually only implied in core video segments. The holistic mining brings significant information redundancy and inaccurate emotional cues. Thus, fine-grained visual cause extraction has a facilitative effect on both emotion perception and emotion-attributed caption generation. To this end, we propose a fine-grained emotion-cause pair extraction framework for emotion-attributed video captioning. Specifically, we learn pair-wise emotion and cause features in two rounds: 1) We propose a Concept-aware Visual Semantic Decomposition module to augment visual features by exploring scene, object, and motion concepts. Besides, to enhance emotional features, we propose a Visual-guided Emotion Interpretable Learning module, which guides emotion refinement with visual temporal dynamics, and augments the interpretable refinement process by reliable VAD-vector constraints. 2) We achieve emotion-cause pair extraction by cross-coupling the visual and emotional features before and after refinement, and leverage contrastive loss to achieve semantic forced alignment. Overall, our approach optimizes complex semantic understanding and emotion perception of videos, leading to a promising performance in emotional captioning. Extensive experiments on three challenging datasets demonstrate the superiority of our approach and each proposed module, e.g., achieving the best performances with +4.4% and +5.4% w.r.t. BLEU-2 and ROUGE-L, respectively, on the EVC-MSVD dataset.

</details>


### [95] [OmniCap-IF: Benchmarking and Improving Instruction Following Abilities for Omni-Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08572)
*Jiahao Wang,An Ping,Yanghai Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Shihao Li,Hanyan Bian,Yichi Ren,Yize Zhang,Han Wang,Haowen Chen,Junze Li,Jiaqi Wang,Yiyang Hu,Zhuze Xu,Zijie Zhang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniCap-IF基准，首个评估全模态大语言模型在复杂指令下生成描述能力的基准，涵盖50种约束类型和1920个样本，发现"格式-内容权衡"现象，并构建了54K指令微调数据集OmniCap-IF-54K及模型OmniCaptioner-IF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注整体视频理解或纯文本指令遵循，未能捕捉多模态与用户约束之间的复杂交互，缺乏对全模态大语言模型在复杂、多方面指令遵循能力上的评估。

Method: 构建OmniCap-IF基准，从格式正确性和内容正确性两个维度评估，涵盖纯视觉、纯音频、音视频三种模态的50种约束类型，融入时间定位评估时空精度。在1920个高质量样本上评估多个主流模型，并构建OmniCap-IF-54K微调数据集训练OmniCaptioner-IF。

Result: 主流模型在1920个样本上表现存在显著差距；发现"格式-内容权衡"现象，即格式复杂性增加会直接降低模型的全模态推理能力；OmniCaptioner-IF在复杂指令遵循和通用全模态描述上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: OmniCap-IF基准有效揭示了现有全模态模型在指令遵循上的不足及格式-内容权衡问题，OmniCaptioner-IF展示了通过针对性微调改善复杂指令遵循能力的可行性。

Abstract: While Omni-modal Large Language Models (OLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in jointly processing audio and visual streams, their ability to strictly adhere to complex, multi-faceted user instructions remains largely unexplored. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on holistic video understanding or text-only instruction following, failing to capture the intricate interplay between modalities and user constraints. To bridge this gap, we introduce OmniCap-IF, the first comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to evaluate instruction-following capabilities in omni-modal captioning. OmniCap-IF incorporates a systematic framework that assesses captions on two dimensions: format correctness and content correctness. Our benchmark encompasses 50 distinct constraint types across pure visual, pure audio, and audio-visual modalities, while integrating Temporal Grounding to assess spatio-temporal precision. Extensive evaluations of prominent models on 1,920 high-quality samples reveal significant performance disparities. Furthermore, our analysis uncovers a critical "format-content tradeoff", demonstrating that increasing formatting complexity directly degrades models' omni-modal reasoning abilities. Finally, to advance the field, we curate a 54K instruction-tuning dataset, OmniCap-IF-54K and present OmniCaptioner-IF, which achieves notable improvements in both complex instruction adherence and general omni-modal captioning performance.

</details>


### [96] [Facial Expression Recognition in the Deep Learning Era: A Systematic Multi-Criteria Review of Methods, Models, Datasets, Performance, Challenges, and Future Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08612)
*Spyridon Georgiou,Aggelos Psiris,Spyridon Evangelatos,Thomas Lagkas,Vasileios Argyriou,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis,Iraklis Varlamis,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述全面梳理了深度学习驱动的面部表情识别（FER）领域的最新进展，将其划分为五个发展阶段（从手工特征到基础模型），从七个互补维度构建了多准则分类体系，并系统汇总了公共数据集、性能指标、当前挑战及未来方向，填补了当前缺乏系统性综述的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习FER综述仅沿狭窄的任务、架构或应用特定轴线进行回顾，缺乏一个整体性、系统组织的近期进展梳理。该文旨在填补这一空白，提供与更广泛的面部情感识别（FAR）领域明确关联的全面综述。

Method: 采用多准则分类法，沿七个互补维度（识别任务、输入模态、人脸预处理流程、网络架构、学习策略、采集设置、应用领域）分析文献，并进行了按准则的对比分析、数据集任务组织回顾、性能指标汇总及代表性方法定量比较。

Result: a) 将FER演化划分为手工特征与经典ML、深度卷积、注意力机制、视觉-语言、基础模型五个阶段；b) 构建七轴分类体系；c) 提供各类别在自然条件下的优劣势洞察；d) 按任务组织汇总公开数据集；e) 给出代表性SOTA方法在广泛采用基准上的定量对比；f) 讨论当前挑战与未来方向。

Conclusion: 该综述系统性地连接了FER与更广泛的FAR领域，为研究者提供了全面的深度学习FER进展图景，识别了当前方法的局限，并指出了有前景的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Facial Expression Recognition (FER) has advanced rapidly over the last decade, driven by the shift from handcrafted descriptors and shallow classifiers to deep convolutional, attention-based, vision-language, and foundation-model architectures, and by the parallel growth of large-scale in-the-wild benchmarks spanning categorical, dimensional, compound, micro-expression, Action Unit (AU), and intensity-estimation tasks. Yet the deep learning-based FER landscape has so far been reviewed only along narrow task-, architecture-, or application-specific axes, leaving a holistic, systematically organized account of its recent advances missing. This survey addresses that gap with a comprehensive review of recent deep learning-based FER, explicitly linked to the wider Facial Affect Recognition (FAR) domain. Its main contributions are: a) A description of FER's evolution into five distinct phases, from handcrafted features and classical machine learning to attention-based, vision-language, and foundation-model approaches, with the key milestone works of each, b) A multi-criteria taxonomy analyzing the literature along seven complementary axes: recognition task, input modality, face pre-processing pipeline, network architecture, learning strategy, acquisition setting, and application domain, c) A per-criterion comparative analysis, with critical insights into the strengths and limitations of each category under in-the-wild conditions, d) A task-organized review of public FER datasets, with their annotation schemes, modalities, and evaluation protocols, e) A compilation of performance metrics and a per-task quantitative comparison of representative state-of-the-art methods on widely adopted benchmarks, and f) A discussion of current challenges and promising future directions.

</details>


### [97] [Harnessing Streaming Video in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08615)
*Dingyu Yao,Shuhuan Gu,Qingyi Si,Junhao Zhou,Chenxu Yang,Chuanyu Qin,Naibin Gu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对VLM在流式视频理解中缺乏主动交互、长时记忆和实时处理能力的问题，从数据集构建（Streaming-Train-248K）、部署系统（Streaming Harness）和评测基准（Streaming-Eval）三方面提出完整解决方案，实验证明该方法在流式视频理解的核心能力上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在离线视频理解上表现优异，但缺乏流式场景所需的主动交互、长时记忆和实时处理能力，且缺少专门的流式部署基础设施，亟需从模型能力、部署系统和评测标准三个层面填补这一空白。

Method: (i) 构建Streaming-Train-248K流式数据集并设计新型训练目标，使VLM适应流式交互和理解；(ii) 提出Streaming Harness即插即用系统，赋予任意VLM三大核心能力：主动交互（每秒响应决策）、长时记忆（12小时上下文保持）和实时处理（亚秒级延迟）；(iii) 设计Streaming-Eval基准评测，覆盖多样化野外场景。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在流式视频理解所需的所有核心能力上均取得一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文的工作推动了VLM从离线视频理解向可部署的流式智能的转变，并将开源数据、代码和基准以促进社区发展。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly required to process unbounded video streams in applications such as video-call assistants, live commentary, and embodied robots. An ideal streaming system should support proactive interaction, long-horizon memory, and real-time processing, while resting on a VLM backbone capable of handling diverse in-the-wild streaming tasks. However, existing VLMs excel at offline video understanding but fall short in streaming capabilities and lack dedicated infrastructure for streaming deployment. We address this gap on three fronts. (i) For backbone capability, we construct \textbf{Streaming-Train-248K}, a streaming dataset paired with a novel training objective for adapting VLMs to streaming interaction and understanding. (ii) For real-world deployment, we introduce \textbf{Streaming Harness}, a plug-and-play system that endows any VLM with three core abilities: proactive interaction (per-second response decisions), long-term memory (12-hour context retention), and real-time processing (sub-second latency). (iii) To drive continued community progress on streaming capabilities, we design \textbf{Streaming-Eval}, a benchmark that reflects models' capabilities across diverse in-the-wild scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains from our approach across all core capabilities required for streaming video understanding. We will open-source our data, code, and benchmark to advance the community's shift from offline video understanding to deployable streaming intelligence.

</details>


### [98] [SSAFE: Simple and Strong AI-Generated Image Detection via Frozen Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08634)
*Seunghyun Lee,Byoungkwon Kim,Jaehyun Nam,Kyungmin Lee,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现冻结的多模态视觉编码器天然能够区分真实与合成图像，结合表征感知的数据筛选策略（仅需10K训练图像）即可实现高效深度伪造检测，在多个基准上优于需要大量数据的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的快速发展模糊了真实与合成图像的边界，现有深度伪造检测方法依赖大规模真实-伪造数据集，但随着新生成器不断涌现，维护这些数据集变得越来越困难。

Method: 1) 利用冻结的多模态编码器提取图像表征，在其嵌入空间中发现真实与合成图像天然可分；2) 提出表征感知的数据筛选策略，选择具有代表性的生成器构成紧凑训练集（仅10K图像）；3) 引入包含现代相机照片、商业图库图片和最新商业生成器输出的RealWorldBench基准。

Result: 仅用10K训练图像即可在多个基准上取得优异性能，对未见生成器和分布偏移具有更强的鲁棒性，优于使用288K（AIGIBench）和4M（OpenFake）数据的方法。

Conclusion: 将冻结的多模态表征与精心筛选的训练数据相结合，为AI生成图像检测提供了一种简单而有效的方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative models has blurred the boundary between synthetic and real imagery, creating an urgent need for reliable deepfake detection. Yet most existing approaches rely on massive real--fake datasets, which are increasingly difficult to maintain as new generators continue to emerge. In this work, we investigate how much information about image authenticity is already encoded in modern multimodal vision representations. We find that frozen multimodal encoders naturally separate real and synthetic images in their embedding space, enabling a simple linear classifier to achieve strong performance without task-specific fine-tuning. Motivated by this observation, we develop a representation-aware data curation strategy that selects a compact set of representative generators for training. The resulting training set contains only 10K images, compared to 288K in AIGIBench and 4M in OpenFake, while improving robustness to unseen generators and distribution shifts. We additionally introduce RealWorldBench, a benchmark consisting of modern camera photographs, contemporary stock images, and outputs from recent commercial generators. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that combining frozen multimodal representations with carefully curated training data provides a simple and effective approach to AI-generated image detection.

</details>


### [99] [Learnable Token Sparsification for Efficient Gigapixel Whole Slide Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08641)
*Jingzhi Chen,Landi He,Zhuo Chen,Shawn Young,Lijian Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可训练的稀疏化框架，用于全切片图像中的视觉token缩减。通过解耦路由架构和SparseLearn组件（含可微分Soft Top-K操作与对角注意力去噪器），在推理时仅保留32个token（原始长度的0.78%），在SlideBench（TCGA）上达到73.32%的准确率，并展现出强零样本泛化能力，为解决千兆像素病理图像的视觉上下文瓶颈提供了高效范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全切片图像处理方案依赖空间下采样或启发式剪枝策略，这些非训练方法会丢弃散落分布但临床重要的病理模式，导致细粒度诊断信息丢失。需要一种可学习的稀疏化方法来替代固定启发式规则。

Method: 提出解耦路由架构；引入SparseLearn组件，包含方差保持噪声门控（通过可微分Soft Top-K操作调节信息流）和对角注意力去噪器（恢复扰动表示而不泄露空间信息）；训练后丢弃SparseLearn，使用确定性Hard Top-K保留最高分的32个token。

Result: 在SlideBench（TCGA）上达到73.32%总体准确率，压缩至原始长度的0.78%（32个token），持续优于基于采样的基线和通用视觉语言模型；在SlideBench（BCNB）和WSI VQA*上表现出强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作通过可学习的token稀疏化策略解决了千兆像素WSI推理中的视觉上下文瓶颈，防止了稀疏诊断证据的稀释，为端到端全切片图像推理提供了一种高效范式。

Abstract: The processing of gigapixel whole slide images within vision language models faces a major difficulty due to an excessive number of visual tokens. Existing solutions typically rely on spatial downsampling or heuristic pruning strategies that operate without training, and these methods often discard subtle but clinically meaningful patterns because pathological evidence is scattered irregularly across the tissue. To overcome this limitation, we reformulate token reduction in whole slide images as a trainable sparsification problem, allowing the model to learn an optimal selection strategy instead of following fixed heuristics. We propose a decoupled routing architecture. To enable gradient propagation through the nondifferentiable pruning operation during training, we introduce a component called SparseLearn. This component uses a variance-preserving noise gate that regulates the information flow of each patch via a differentiable Soft Top-K operator, together with a diagonal attention denoiser that recovers perturbed representations without leaking spatial information. At inference time, the SparseLearn module is entirely discarded, and the trained scorer applies a deterministic Hard Top-K operator to keep only the highest scoring 32 tokens, incurring no extra computation. By compressing the visual sequence down to a sparse set of just 32 tokens, which represents as little as 0.78% of the original length, our framework achieves 73.32% overall accuracy on SlideBench (TCGA), consistently surpassing sampling-based baselines and general-purpose vision language models. It also demonstrates strong zero shot generalization on SlideBench (BCNB) and WSI VQA*. By resolving the visual context bottleneck and preventing the dilution of sparse diagnostic evidence, this work provides a highly efficient paradigm for end to end gigapixel whole slide image reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [FiberTune: Preserving Action-Fiber Visual Residuals in Vision-Language-Action Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08653)
*Haihao Lin,Xiangsheng Huang,Xiao Yang,Weibang Zhou,Yiqi Zhang,Bo Yang,Simin Zeng,Jiawei Yang,Zhengyang Wang,Jiahui Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FiberTune提出了一种训练目标，通过在动作等效状态下保持教师结构的视觉残差，防止VLA策略微调中的视觉表征坍塌，在不增加推理开销的情况下提升了多个基准的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的动作监督VLA微调仅约束影响预测动作的方向，导致视觉表征在动作等价的局部纤维上发生残差视觉坍塌（residual visual collapse），即不同视觉但动作等价的状态共享了退化的视觉特征，限制了策略的泛化能力。

Method: FiberTune在训练时引入在线动作探针（online action probe）估计动作预测性特征方向，将其从中间视觉token表征中过滤掉，然后将探针过滤后的残差与冻结的视觉教师模型对齐，并正则化其有效秩（effective rank）。

Result: 在六项受控仿真设置（两个基准、两种架构pi_0.5和OpenVLA-OFT）及物理SO-101拾取放置任务中，FiberTune全面超越仅使用任务损失微调的基线。代表性增益：CALVIN ABC-to-D长期任务SR(5)提升+10.7个百分点，SO-101物理任务成功率从72.7%提升至78.1%。

Conclusion: 残差诊断表明性能提升与探针过滤残差的教师对齐度和有效秩增加一致，验证了动作纤维动机，证明FiberTune能有效缓解动作等价状态下的视觉表征坍塌问题。

Abstract: Action-supervised fine-tuning of vision-language-action (VLA) policies fits demonstrations effectively but constrains only the directions that change predicted actions, leaving visual structure consistent across action-equivalent states free to collapse. We formalize this as residual visual collapse along local action fibers and propose FiberTune, a training-time objective that preserves teacher-structured visual residuals without adding inference-time overhead. FiberTune uses an online action probe to estimate action-predictive feature directions, filters them from intermediate visual-token representations, and aligns the resulting probe-filtered residuals to a frozen visual teacher while regularizing their effective rank. Under identical training conditions, FiberTune improves over task-loss-only fine-tuning in every one of six controlled simulation settings spanning two benchmarks and two architectures (pi_0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT), as well as on physical SO-101 pick-place; representative gains include +10.7 percentage points SR(5) on long-horizon CALVIN ABC-to-D and physical SO-101 task success rising from 72.7% to 78.1%. Residual diagnostics show that these gains coincide with increased probe-filtered residual teacher alignment and effective rank, consistent with the action-fiber motivation.

</details>


### [101] [WaveDiT: Distribution-Aware Wavelet Flow Matching for Efficient 3D Brain MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08670)
*Danilo Danese,Angela Lombardi,Giuseppe Fasano,Matteo Attimonelli,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaveDiT 提出了一种基于3D Haar离散小波变换系数空间的条件流匹配框架，通过分解式空间-深度注意力与基于高阶小波统计量的带异质异方差不确定性建模，在单GPU上实现全分辨率3D脑MRI合成，在多项评估指标上优于扩散、潜变量和小波基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大尺度且人口学平衡的数据集对可靠的神经影像生物标志物至关重要，全分辨率3D脑MRI合成可支持数据增强，但现有方法要么在体素尺度上计算成本过高，要么依赖有损的潜在压缩而损害解剖细节，且实用的3D生成增强通常需要专门的计算基础设施。

Method: 提出WaveDiT，在3D Haar离散小波变换系数空间中运行的条件流匹配框架。结合了分解式空间-深度注意力（factorized spatio-depth attention）与基于高阶小波统计量导出的带异质异方差不确定性建模（band-wise heteroscedastic uncertainty modeling）。将预测的对数方差直接集成到流目标和条件路径中，实现与解剖细节的重尾且依赖于输入的方差结构相一致的自适应精度。

Result: 在单GPU上实现全分辨率3D合成，满足实际内存和时间约束。在多中心队列上的评估表明，与扩散、潜变量和小波基线方法相比，生成与真实MRI分布的对齐度提升，下游脑龄预测性能增强，区域级解剖一致性也得到改善。

Conclusion: WaveDiT在全分辨率3D脑MRI合成中实现了性能与计算效率的良好平衡，为实际应用中的数据增强提供了可行方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large and demographically balanced datasets are essential for reliable neuroimaging biomarkers. Full-resolution 3D brain MRI synthesis can support data augmentation in this setting, but existing approaches either incur prohibitive computational cost at volumetric scale or rely on lossy latent compression that may compromise anatomical detail. As a result, practical 3D generative augmentation often requires specialized compute infrastructure. We propose WaveDiT, a conditional flow matching framework operating in the coefficient space of a 3D Haar Discrete Wavelet Transform. The model combines factorized spatio-depth attention with band-wise heteroscedastic uncertainty modeling derived from higher-order wavelet statistics. Predicted log-variance is integrated directly into both the flow objective and conditioning pathway, enabling adaptive precision consistent with the heavy-tailed and input-dependent variance structure of anatomical detail. This formulation supports full-resolution 3D synthesis under practical memory and time constraints on a single modern GPU. Evaluation on a multi-site cohort demonstrates improved alignment between generated and real MRI distributions, together with enhanced downstream brain age prediction and region-level anatomical agreement relative to diffusion, latent, and wavelet-based baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/sisinflab/WaveDiT

</details>


### [102] [Learning to Solve Generative ODEs Beyond the Linear Span](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08672)
*Sihyeon Kim,Seunghun Lee,Vikas Singh,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpanLift，一种轻量级神经求解器，通过空间残差算子增强标量系数更新，突破了传统求解器学习的跨度限制瓶颈，在极少量步数下实现了SOTA采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流生成模型需要大量顺序模型评估才能获得高质量样本。现有求解器学习方法（调整标量系数、时间步长）存在结构性瓶颈：每一步的更新受限于跨度（span），标量系数更新只能拟合速度评估张成空间内的分量，而无法触及张成空间外的残差。

Method: SpanLift将固定基求解器作为跨内先验，并学习一个作用于状态和速度缓冲区上的空间残差算子。该算子通过端点教师匹配（endpoint teacher matching）训练，保持预训练骨干网络不变，且不增加模型NFE。

Result: 在像素空间扩散、潜空间流匹配和降水临近预报等任务上，SpanLift实现了SOTA的少步采样。仅用3步NFE，CIFAR-10 FID从8.16降至5.69，ImageNet FID从17.37降至11.83。学习到的校正主要位于张成空间外，且可跨基求解器迁移。

Conclusion: SpanLift通过引入空间残差算子有效解决了求解器学习的跨度限制问题，在保持预训练骨干不变且不增加计算开销的前提下显著提升了少步采样质量，并具有良好的可迁移性。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow generative models sample by integrating a learned ODE, but high quality still requires many sequential model evaluations. Solver learning reduces this cost by adapting scalar coefficients, timesteps, or both, while keeping the backbone model fixed. In this work, we identify a structural bottleneck in this update family: each step remains span-limited. Since the scalar-coefficient update lies in the span of buffered velocity evaluations, it can fit only the in-span component while leaving any out-of-span residual unreachable by scalar recombination alone. We propose SpanLift, a lightweight neural solver that augments scalar-coefficient updates with a spatial residual operator. SpanLift keeps a fixed base solver as an in-span prior and learns a spatial residual operator over the state and velocity buffer. The operator is trained by endpoint teacher matching, preserves the pretrained backbone, and adds no model NFEs. Empirically, the learned correction transfers across base solvers and is predominantly out-of-span. Across pixel-space diffusion, latent flow matching, and precipitation nowcasting, SpanLift achieves state-of-the-art few-step sampling. With only 3 NFE, it improves CIFAR-10 FID from 8.16 to 5.69 and ImageNet FID from 17.37 to 11.83.

</details>


### [103] [BioVid: Autoregressive Video Generation with Biological Behavior Semantic Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08674)
*Tsung-Wei Pan,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BioVid 是一个数据驱动的自回归视频生成框架，通过 FSQ-R3GAN 分词器和因果 Transformer 学习生物行为的自然时间长度分布，在人类饮水行为数据集上生成的时长分布与真实数据高度吻合（Wasserstein-1 距离 1.24），优于固定长度基线（6.05）和 VideoGPT（15.48）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法将时长作为外部预设参数（固定帧数或文本提示），生成的片段时间边界与真实行为数据的统计结构脱节，这与生物行为中动作时长自然变化的特性根本不一致。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用 FSQ-R3GAN（结合 R3GAN 的稳定相对对抗训练目标与 FSQ 的保证码本利用率）将每帧编码为紧凑离散表示；第二阶段使用因果 Transformer 自回归建模 token 序列，通过学习 End-of-Sequence (EOS) token 在行为事件达到语义闭合时终止生成，时长分布完全从训练数据中涌现。

Result: 在 NTU RGB+D (A001, n=94) 人类饮水行为数据集上，BioVid 生成时长分布与真实数据的 Wasserstein-1 距离为 1.24，远优于固定长度基线（6.05）和 VideoGPT（15.48），同时保持有竞争力的空间保真度。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的时长学习策略能够更准确地捕捉生物行为的时间统计结构，EOS token 机制为可变长度视频生成提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Existing video generation frameworks treat sequence duration as an externally prescribed parameter -- fixed frame counts or text prompts -- producing clips whose temporal boundaries are decoupled from the statistical structure of real behavioral data. This assumption is fundamentally misaligned with biological behavior, where action duration varies naturally across individuals and instances and is encoded in the data itself. We present BioVid, a data-driven autoregressive video generation framework that learns the temporal structure of biological behaviors directly from training data, including their natural length distributions. In the first stage, a Finite Scalar Quantization GAN (FSQ-R3GAN) tokenizer encodes each video frame into a compact discrete representation, combining the stabilized relativistic training objective of R3GAN with FSQ's guaranteed codebook utilization to achieve high-fidelity spatial reconstruction without codebook collapse. In the second stage, a causal Transformer models the resulting token sequences autoregressively and learns to emit an End-of-Sequence (EOS) token when the behavioral event reaches semantic closure, with the termination distribution emerging naturally from the training data rather than any human-specified constraint. Experiments on a human drinking behavior dataset (NTU RGB+D, A001, n=94) demonstrate that BioVid's generated length distribution closely matches that of held-out test data, achieving a Wasserstein-1 distance of 1.24 against the ground truth -- compared to 6.05 for a fixed-length baseline and 15.48 for VideoGPT -- while maintaining competitive spatial fidelity.

</details>


### [104] [Distortion-Aware PETR for BEV Object Detection with Mixed Pinhole-Fisheye Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08680)
*Xiangzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAPETR，一种无需投影的鱼眼-针孔混合相机3D目标检测器，通过可学习的畸变感知位置编码和双向特征-几何协同调制模块，在KITTI-360基准上超越基线方法，并揭示了可学习适应与显式几何重参数化之间存在负交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼眼相机在自动驾驶中成本低、视场全覆盖，但其在3D目标检测中的潜力未被充分挖掘。严重的径向畸变破坏了大多数BEV检测器依赖的均匀采样假设，现有方法难以有效处理混合针孔-鱼眼相机设置。

Method: 提出DAPETR框架，包含两个可学习自适应模块：1）统一畸变感知位置编码（Unified Distortion-Aware Positional Embedding），将鱼眼几何信息融入图像表示的位置编码；2）双向特征-几何协同调制模块（Bidirectional Feature-Geometry Co-Modulation），实现图像特征与3D位置嵌入的相互适应。

Result: 在修改后的KITTI-360基准上，DAPETR相比基线PETR和极坐标版本的PolarPETR均取得更优性能；同时发现可学习适应与显式几何重参数化（极坐标表示）结合时存在负交互，两方面策略会相互冲突。

Conclusion: DAPETR推动了鱼眼BEV检测的研究和基准进展，关键洞见是除图像矫正之外，有效的畸变感知3D感知设计需要谨慎处理可学习适应与显式几何建模之间的关系。

Abstract: Fisheye cameras are widely deployed in autonomous driving perception suites for their low cost and full-coverage field of view (FOV), yet their potential remains underleveraged in 3D object detection. Severe radial distortion challenges most BEV detectors by violating the fundamental assumption of uniform sampling. To bridge this gap, we propose Distortion-Aware PETR (DAPETR), a projection-free detector tailored for mixed pinhole-fisheye camera setups. DAPETR incorporates two key learned-adaptive modules: a unified distortion-aware positional embedding that harmonizes positional encodings for image representations with fisheye geometry, and a bidirectional feature-geometry co-modulation module that mutually adapts image features and 3D positional embeddings. In our experiments on a converted KITTI-360 benchmark, we systematically compare our learned adaptive approach against PETR in polar coordinates (PolarPETR). We find that while both methods improve over the baseline, our learned modules achieve superior performance. Crucially, we uncover a negative interaction when combining both strategies, revealing that learned adaptation and explicit geometric reparameterization can conflict. Our final DAPETR model significantly advances the research and benchmark for fisheye BEV detection, providing critical insights into effective distortion-aware 3D perception design other than image rectification.

</details>


### [105] [BLUE: Toward Better Language Use in Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08684)
*George Ling,Lijin Yang,Hao Yang,Zhongzhan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BLUE提出一种轻量级门控机制，通过冻结VLA隐藏状态决定每帧是否生成语言描述，在不修改骨干网络的情况下实现高效语言增强自动驾驶，达到SOTA性能并提升2.54倍推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在每一帧都生成语言描述效率低下，因为语言只在少数路线上有益，且多数计算浪费在不需要语言的帧上。同时，场景复杂度和运动学特征难以预测语言是否有用，但预训练VLA隐藏状态已隐含这一信息。

Method: BLUE在冻结的VLA隐藏状态上训练一个仅0.11M参数的轻量级门控网络，逐帧决定是否激活语言生成或直接预测动作，无需修改骨干网络或额外人工标注。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上达76.2%成功率，在Longest6 v2上达36驾驶分数，较骨干网络实现2.54倍推理加速和8.9%成功率提升。

Conclusion: VLA模型可以以极低计算成本保留语言增强的收益，BLUE为高效语言增强自动驾驶提供了实用路径。

Abstract: We present BLUE, a minimal method for better language use in vision-language-action (VLA) models for autonomous driving (AD). Through extensive analysis, we reveal that language matters on only a small fraction of routes, but on those routes it can greatly improve or degrade performance. Generating language at every frame is therefore inefficient, since most computation is spent on frames that do not benefit from language. We further show that pretrained VLA hidden states potentially already encode whether language will benefit a given frame, even though scene complexity and kinematic features alone struggle to predict this. Based on this finding, BLUE trains a lightweight gate on frozen VLA hidden states to decide per frame whether to activate language generation or predict actions directly, without modifying the backbone or requiring additional human annotation. With just a 0.11M-parameter gate, BLUE sets a new state of the art on both benchmarks, achieving 76.2% success rate on Bench2Drive and 36 driving score on Longest6 v2, while delivering 2.54x inference speedup and 8.9% success rate improvement over the backbone. BLUE provides a practical path toward efficient language-augmented AD, showing that VLA models can retain the benefits of language at a fraction of the cost. Our code, data, logs and checkpoints are fully available on https://github.com/George-Ling3/BLUE.

</details>


### [106] [Shift-Dependent Asymmetry: Orthogonal Inverse Low-Rank Adaptation for Federated Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08687)
*Xingyue Zhao,Wenke Huang,Linghao Zhuang,Haoran Wu,Anwen Jiang,Zhifeng Wang,Wenwen He,Ming Feng,Mang Ye,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对联邦LoRA在医学图像分割中编解码器不对称导致泛化差的问题，提出IAT方法：通过模块个性化吸收外观偏移和监督差异，并结合子空间正交正则化防止共享路径中的信息泄露，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA方法采用统一聚合规则，忽视了医学分割中编码器（受外观偏移主导）与解码器（受监督差异主导）的不对称性，导致共享解剖特征与站点特定偏差纠缠，损害泛化能力。

Method: 提出Inverse Asymmetric Tuning (IAT)方法：(1) 对编码器和解码器进行模块级个性化，分别吸收外观偏移和站点依赖的监督差异，同时保留共享路径传递共识；(2) 引入子空间正交正则化器（Subspace Orthogonality Regularizer），惩罚共享方向与局部方向在有效更新空间中的共线性，缓解LoRA双线性参数化下站点特定更新的泄露问题。

Result: 在多个强联邦LoRA和参数高效FL基线上实现了一致性的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过解耦编解码器异构性并正则化共享-局部子空间正交性，有效缓解了联邦医学分割中多站点偏差对共享表示的污染，是一种高效且无需额外通信开销的联邦微调方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient federated fine-tuning of segmentation foundation models for medical imaging. However, most federated LoRA methods adopt a uniform aggregation rule, which breaks under the encoder-decoder asymmetry in medical segmentation: the encoder is dominated by appearance shifts, while the decoder is dominated by supervision variations. This mismatch entangles shared anatomy with site-specific biases and harms generalization. To address this, we propose Inverse Asymmetric Tuning (IAT). IAT aligns adaptation with heterogeneity sources by personalizing module-specific components in the encoder to absorb appearance shifts and in the decoder to accommodate site-dependent supervision, while retaining a shared pathway for transferable consensus. However, structural separation alone is insufficient under LoRA's bilinear parameterization, where multiplicative coupling can still cause site-specific updates to leak into the shared direction. We therefore introduce a Subspace Orthogonality Regularizer that penalizes shared-local collinearity in the effective update space, mitigating leakage without extra communication. Experiments show consistent improvements over strong federated LoRA and parameter-efficient FL baselines.

</details>


### [107] [PRPO: Perception-Reinforced Policy Optimization via Token-Level Dynamic Advantage Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08708)
*Qiming Li,Tianlun Li,Xiaolong Cheng,Hangyu Li,Ruiyan Gong,Kangning Niu,Kaitao Jiang,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRPO提出了一种token级强化学习框架，通过显式识别和强化多模态推理轨迹中关键的感知token，解决了现有RLVR方法中粗粒度信用分配问题，在7个多模态推理基准上取得了平均23.3%（3B模型）和21.1%（7B模型）的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅依赖轨迹级结果奖励，对所有生成token分配相同的学习信号。在多模态推理中，只有稀疏的子集token与视觉证据因果相关，导致关键感知token被语言先验或推理模板token淹没，监督信号薄弱。

Method: 提出Robust Visual Dependency (RVD)指标来识别既视觉依赖又扰动稳定的token，并基于RVD设计Perceptual Advantage Reshaping (PAR)机制，实现token级信用分配：放大感知信息丰富的token梯度，同时保持非感知token的稳定梯度。

Result: 在7个多模态推理基准上，3B和7B模型分别取得平均23.3%和21.1%的提升，达到最先进水平，并展现出更优的训练效率和跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 细粒度的token级信用分配对于可扩展的多模态强化学习至关重要，PRPO验证了该方向的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become an effective paradigm for improving the reasoning capability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). However, existing RLVR methods primarily rely on trajectory-level outcome rewards, which assign identical learning signals across all generated tokens. This coarse-grained credit assignment is fundamentally mismatched to multimodal reasoning, where only a sparse subset of tokens is causally grounded in visual evidence. Consequently, these pivotal perceptual tokens receive weak supervision and are often overwhelmed by language priors or reasoning-template tokens. To address this limitation, we propose Perception-Reinforced Policy Optimization (PRPO), a token-level reinforcement learning framework that explicitly identifies and reinforces pivotal perceptual tokens within long-horizon multimodal reasoning trajectories. PRPO introduces Robust Visual Dependency (RVD), a principled metric that identifies tokens whose predictions are both visually grounded and perturbation-stable, filtering out brittle or noisy visual tokens. Based on RVD, we further propose Perceptual Advantage Reshaping (PAR), a token-level credit assignment technique that amplifies perceptually informative tokens while preserving stable gradients for non-perceptual tokens. Extensive experiments on seven multimodal reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PRPO consistently outperforms strong LVLM baselines across both 3B and 7B model scales, achieving average gains of 23.3% and 21.1%, respectively. PRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance with improved training efficiency and stronger cross-task generalization. Our findings highlight the importance of fine-grained credit assignment for scalable multimodal reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [108] [Thinking Without Images: Internalizing Visual Manipulation with On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08719)
*Yishuo Cai,Jiahui Liu,Yuanxin Liu,Haobo Deng,Linli Yao,Yuhao Zheng,Kun Ouyang,Zhimo Li,Ziyue Wang,Xu Sun,Haoli Bai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Imagine-OPD框架，通过“想象推理”（Thinking with Imagination）内化“图像推理”（Thinking with Images）的视觉推理能力，在保持性能的同时显著降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "Thinking with Images"范式虽能通过显式缩放和裁剪区域提升细粒度视觉推理能力，但带来了冗余工具调用和更长推理轨迹的问题，且从结果奖励中学习到的中间裁剪或视觉线索可能包含噪声，无法准确捕捉任务相关的视觉证据。

Method: 提出Imagine-OPD，一种在策略自蒸馏框架：训练阶段教师扮演"Thinking with Images"推理者，接收来自标注区域的特权缩放视图，并监督模型自身的想象推理轨迹；无需外部教师或高质量想象演示。

Result: 在视觉中心基准测试中，Imagine-OPD在对比模型中取得最佳平均性能，同时相较于"Thinking with Images"方法显著降低了推理开销。

Conclusion: 视觉推理的"想象"能力可以通过内部化过程有效学习，无需实际工具调用即可获得类似甚至更好的性能，为高效细粒度视觉推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: ''Thinking with Images'' has emerged as an effective paradigm for fine-grained visual reasoning: by explicitly zooming into relevant regions and reasoning over crops, models can access local evidence that is difficult to recover from a single global image. However, this benefit comes with redundant tool invocations and longer inference traces. Moreover, when such behaviors are learned mainly from outcome reward, the resulting intermediate crops or visual cues can be noisy or fail to faithfully capture task-relevant visual evidence. In this work, we ask whether the reasoning benefits of ''Thinking with Images'' can be internalized through Thinking with Imagination: an internal process that decides where to look and imagines what visual cues closer inspection would reveal without actually invoking tools. We propose Imagine-OPD, an on-policy self-distillation framework in which a teacher plays the role of a ''Thinking with Images'' reasoner during training: it receives privileged zoomed evidence views derived from annotated regions, and supervises the model's own imagination reasoning trajectories. Imagine-OPD does not require an external teacher or high-quality imagination demonstrations.
